This section says what the website does with it. It was built on the made-up city, whose count names no real group.

**Where it is, and when it is asked for.**

| Point | Detail |
|---|---|
| The part | The fifth of the parts that are closed until they are pressed, after the sources and before "Go and look". Its id is `census`, and the list of contents leads to it. It comes after every figure of the place and stands beside none of them |
| What the page holds as it is built | The heading and the words that offer it, which are `census` of route 11 and hold no figure. No figure is in the page, its title, its description or what it hands its scripts. The part carries `data-nosnippet`. `check:pages` reads every built page for a figure of the census |
| When it is asked for | When the part is opened: by a press, by Enter or Space on its heading, or by a link that names it. Route 13 is called once, with the slug of the page and nothing else, `no-store`. Closed and opened again, the answer in hand is shown |
| What is kept | Nothing. The answer is held while the page is open. The part is closed again on every visit, and whether it was opened is written nowhere |
| With scripts off | The part opens, and says that the figures are loaded when asked for, which needs scripts. It is the one part of an area's page that does not read with scripts off, and `/accessibility` says so |
| Where no census is served | Route 11 says `available` false, and the part is not drawn. Where the page was built while one was served and the service has since stopped, the part says so in the API's words, and offers no second try |
| Once the figures have come | The day of the census, five notes, and one line that says what the picture is. Then each table, closed under its own name until it is pressed, as the browser's own `details`. Then where the figures came from and how they were made |

**The rules, and what holds each.** `src/components/CensusPanel/` is the part, and `test/census/` holds the rules.

| # | Rule | Held by |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **It is a table first.** Every figure is in a cell, in words. A table has a caption, a heading for each column and a heading for each row. The caption says what the table is, which census it is from, its day, the area it is for and how many were counted, so that a picture of one table carries all of it | `test_every_table_is_a_table_with_its_caption_and_its_headings`, axe |
| 2 | **The picture is second, and says nothing the table does not.** Beside a share is the same share drawn: a bar for the area and a mark for the whole city. It stands after the words, and is kept from a screen reader. Where neither share is a figure, nothing is drawn | `test_the_picture_says_nothing_the_table_does_not`, `test_a_row_with_no_share_to_draw_draws_no_picture` |
| 3 | **No colour says good or bad.** Every colour of the part is one the text of the page is drawn in. No rule of its style sheet draws one row or one table unlike another: a step in is the only mark of a row under a group. Every bar is one colour and one height, whatever it holds | `test_every_colour_is_one_the_text_of_the_page_is_drawn_in`, `test_no_rule_draws_one_row_or_one_table_unlike_another`, `test_every_bar_is_drawn_alike_whatever_it_holds` |
| 4 | **A figure stands beside the whole city's figure for the same thing, and beside nothing else.** A table has four columns and no more: the row, the share in the area, the number counted, and the share in the city. No other area is named in the part, and no link in it leads to an area, a search or a comparison. Nothing of a search stands in it: no rank, no fit, no journey, no budget | `test_every_table_has_four_columns_and_no_more`, `test_no_other_area_is_named_and_none_is_linked_to`, `test_nothing_of_a_search_stands_in_it` |
| 5 | **Nothing puts one row before another.** The rows are drawn in the order they came, which is the publisher's, and that is not the order of their figures. There is nothing to sort, filter or switch by | `test_the_rows_are_drawn_in_the_order_they_came`, `test_the_rows_are_not_in_the_order_of_their_figures`, `test_there_is_nothing_to_sort_filter_or_switch_by` |
| 6 | **No word is one Burro would be choosing.** Every word about a figure is the API's. The site copy names a control or a state, holds no figure, names no group, and holds none of "diverse", "mixed", "main", "majority", "minority", "more", "less" and the like | `test_the_site_copy_says_nothing_of_what_a_figure_means`, `test_the_site_copy_holds_no_figure_and_names_no_group`, and the same of the API's words in core |
| 7 | **A small number is said in words.** A share under 1 in 100 reads "fewer than 1 in 100", and its count reads "Not given". Nothing reads "0%" or "none" | `test_a_share_under_one_in_a_hundred_is_said_in_words_and_gives_no_count` |
| 8 | **A table that is left out says so, and nothing more.** Where too few were counted, or the census holds nothing for the area, the sentence the API sent stands in the place of the table. No figure is filled in | `test_a_table_that_is_left_out_says_so_and_nothing_more` |
| 9 | **Every table is one press from its source.** Under a table: what it counts in the publisher's words, and the way to its source on the page of sources. Where the API names the publisher's page for the table, the way to that too, which tells the publisher nothing of the page the person came from | `test_every_table_is_one_press_from_its_source`, `test_the_publishers_page_is_linked_where_the_api_names_one`, `test_an_address_that_is_no_web_address_is_never_made_a_link` |
| 10 | **Only the page of an area asks for it.** One component calls route 13 and one page draws it. A build never reads it. The part reads nothing of a search, a comparison, a vibe or the map. No other answer the website reads holds a row of the census | `test/census/apart.test.ts` |

**On a wide screen and on a phone.** Measured on 2026-09-24 in a browser, against the service on the made-up release, in CSS pixels, on the page of one area whose five tables hold 83 rows.

| | Desktop, 1440 by 900 | Phone, 390 by 844 |
|---|---|---|
| The page, with nothing open | 2,821 | 5,018 |
| The page, with the part open and every table closed | 3,588 | 6,111 |
| One table, open | 691 to 1,362 | 1,172 to 2,495 |
| The page, with every table open | 8,472 | 15,062 |
| A table | 720 wide, in one column: the row, then 176, 104 and 104 for the three figures | As wide as the page, 358. Each row is stacked: the row first, and under it the three figures side by side, each under its own name |
| A row | 41 high, and 65 to 89 where its name wraps | 86 high, and 110 to 134 where its name wraps |
| The picture | Beside its share, 96 wide and 12 high | The same, in a third of the width of the page |
| What opens the part, and each table | 44 high | 44 or 45 high |
| The page scrolls sideways | No | No |

- **Each table is closed under its own name.** With every table open at once the part was thirteen screens of a phone. Now what is opened is what a person chose to read.
- **The picture stands beside its share, not under it.** Under it, a row was 57 high on a desktop and 102 on a phone.
- **"fewer than 1 in 100" wraps inside its column.** It ran out of the last column and past the table.
- **A row with no share to draw draws nothing.** A line alone stood under "fewer than 1 in 100", and said nothing.
- **What a link hands from the page to the part is plain text.** The id of the part was taken from the file of the part, which runs in the browser, and the built page linked to a function in its place. It is in a file of its own, and a test reads what the page takes from the other.
- **Opened by keyboard, the focus stays on what opened it**, and the figures are said to be loading in a status. One request is made, to route 13, and none to any other host. Nothing is in local storage, session storage or a cookie, and the address does not change.
- **Not seen: a screen reader, 200% zoom, forced colours, and the dark theme.** Section 9 lists them as checks to make by hand.

### 2.2 The household income of an area

The page of an area holds a second part about who lives there: what the households of the area are estimated to have as income ([ADR 0028](../adr/0028-household-income-is-shown-and-never-ranked-on.md), contract 9.7). It stands after the census figures and is held to the same rules, which `test/income/` holds.

| | |
|---|---|
| Where it stands | After the census figures, before "Go and look", under the heading route 11 gives. The list of contents names it |
| When it is asked for | When the part is opened. Route 14 is called once, with the slug of the page and nothing else. The answer is never kept, and is in no page as it is built |
| What is drawn | The name of the kind of income and the figure. The limits round it. The year. That it is an estimate from a model. The publisher's definition and caveats. The credit, the licence, and a link to the publisher's page where there is one. Every word and every figure is the API's |
| What is never drawn | A bar, a scale, a colour, another area, a figure of the whole city, a rank, or a word for how well off an area is |
| Who reads it | `IncomePanel`, and nothing else. No file of the search, the comparison, the session, the vibes or the map names it, and no recorded ranking, comparison or reason holds a figure of it |

### 2.3 The pages of accounts

Accounts were built on 2026-09-26, and are off until they are turned on ([ADR 0043](../adr/0043-burro-has-accounts-and-a-person-signs-in-by-a-link-sent-by-email.md)). **[The design of accounts](accounts.md) is their source**, for the website as for the service: its section 10 has every page and part, and its section 5 what the website passes on. What stands here is what they change of this document.

| | |
|---|---|
| With accounts off | The website is what the rest of this document says. `NEXT_PUBLIC_BURRO_ACCOUNTS` turns them on, and is read when the website is built. With anything but `on` in it, no page of accounts is reached, the name board and the search page draw nothing of them, and the handler passes nothing on |
| The pages | `/sign-in`, `/sign-in/sent`, `/sign-in/confirm` and `/account`. Each is built the same for everybody, holds nothing of anybody, and is never indexed |
| The parts | One entry of the name board, "Sign in" or "Account". One button on the search page, "Save this search", beside the way to share a search, after the first result |
| The handler | `src/app/v1/auth/` and `src/app/v1/me/`, over `src/lib/account/pass.ts`. It passes a closed list of seventeen routes on to the API, and their answers back. It is the one thing of the website that runs on its host for a person, and not while a page is built |
| What passes through the website's server | What is sent for an account: the address a person signs in with, the token of a link, two cookies, and the spec of a search that is kept. Never a sentence, and never what is typed into the box that finds a place |
| A search that was kept | Is opened by loading its spec into the search that is open in the tab, ranked now, as the spec of a shared link is. Rule 2 of section 0 holds: it is a spec that the API returned |
| The policy | Did not change. The routes of accounts are asked of the website's own origin |

## 3. Every state of the search page

`phase` is one of `empty`, `interpreting`, `results` and `refining`. The other rows are conditions that sit on a phase, and several can hold at once. The map and the way to the table of all areas are on screen in every state. The prompt box is on screen in every state but one: before a search, while Deep search is chosen, it is in the panel that is not drawn. The settings are one press away in every state: before a search they are the panel of Deep search, where they stand open, and once a search is open they are the fold named "Refine search". Until 2026-09-26 they stood open beside the answer from 80rem, and under it were behind the helper "Choose without typing" before a search and behind their own button after one.

What a state says, in which words and in what order, is as it was before the look. The table says how each is drawn where that tells one state from another. A notice is cream within a rule of ink, with a band of amber at its start. A failure is the same with a band of poppy, and every word of it is in ink. Neither has a shadow, and neither is a solid ground of colour.

| State | Entered when | The person sees | The person can |
|---|---|---|---|
| Empty | No search yet | Step 1 of section 1. Quick search is chosen. The settings hold the defaults of the kind of search that is chosen, which is `defaults.rent` at first, in the panel of Deep search, with their first two groups open. There are no chips and no list. Burro sits beside the heading, and moves | Type. Open a helper, and then pick an example, or open a word of the shelf, look at the map by it and add that vibe. Choose Deep search, and then say whether they rent, buy or visit, add a place, set what matters to them, and make a search of it by its button. Find an area by its name. Browse areas |
| Interpreting | Text was sent to route 1 | Step 3. The text stays in the box. Burro hops where the results will stand, in one box, and nothing else holds the place of what is coming: no grey bar and no grey card. On a first search what Burro understood is not drawn until the words are read. It lasts until the ranking of the words is in, and on a search that began with no result in sight no less than the wait (section 1): what was understood is then shown with the answer | Stop, which aborts the request and puts the search back as it was when the sentence was sent: the spec, its chips, and what was read before. The list was never changed. The text stays in the box, and the line under it says that Burro stopped, until the search next moves |
| Results | Route 2 answered with at least one `ranked` | Chips, list, map, with everything that opens closed (section 4.1). One line of site copy for each `UnmetCategory` in `unmet`. One line for each `RejectReason` in `rejected`, beside the chip it concerns, or under the chips when it has none | Read, open sources, open the working of a result, see ten more, refine, compare, share, hide an area, open an area page or the areas most like it |
| Refining | A chip, a control or a second sentence changed something | The last results, marked `aria-busy`, under the words "Working out the ranking again". They are not dimmed: at 70% the muted text, the links and the edges all fall below the contrast of section 8. The control touched shows its new value. When the answer comes every control is redrawn from the returned spec, and the live region says how many areas changed place | Keep editing. The latest request wins: an older answer is dropped by its sequence number |
| A name that several places bear | `clarify` is not empty | **Nothing is asked.** Results for everything that was read, the journey among them. The page takes the first of the places the service gives: it copies the edit at `operations[group][index]`, puts the `id` of that place in it, and sends it with the rest (`settledOf` in `src/lib/search/takes.ts`). The chip of the journey names the place that was taken, and says "assumed". Where the service gives no place with a name like the one that was typed, nothing is taken, and the line of what was left out names "A journey" and says, one press away, that Burro does not know the place and where another is named. Until 2026-09-27 a line of its own under the chips said "Burro does not know that place.", of nothing. A name that several areas bear settles nothing: a rule for an area hides it, or leaves every other out, and none is set on a guess. Until the evening of 2026-09-26 the page asked, in a dialogue box over the chips: "Which place did you mean?", then up to five choices, the way to the field that finds a place, and "Leave it out". With three places it was 304 px high on a desktop and 275 on a phone, and the first result began at 776 of 900 on a desktop. The founder asked that Burro asks nothing ([ADR 0012](../adr/0012-a-closed-vocabulary-and-a-guard-on-the-model.md), as amended) | Change the place in the group of journeys, under "Refine search", or take the chip off |
| Nothing matches | Route 2 answered and `ranked` is empty | A box where the first result would stand. Where a limit left an area out: "No area passes every limit you set." Where none did: "Burro could not rank any area, because it has too few figures for what counts in your search.", and what has no figure, by the names the API gives. A limit is never blamed that nobody set. A count for each `FilterReason` and `UnrankedReason`. One button for each firm limit in the spec, where a limit left an area out: make the budget flexible, make a journey flexible, show hidden areas. The map hatched, and the table with each area's reason and what it has no figure for | Press a button, which sends that one edit |
| Nothing read | `status` is `off_topic`, or nothing came of the words: no edit changed the search, the page took nothing of what was noticed and left nothing, nothing was heard that Burro has no data on, which is to say that `unmet` holds `other` or nothing, and no model is still reading the words. A wish that Burro has no data on was heard: the page says a line of each under what it understood, and never over them that nothing could be read | `notice_text` if there is one, else one line of site copy, which says what Burro reads and gives a sentence that it does read: a sentence in another language was met with "Nothing in that could be read as a setting", which led nowhere. A test holds the sentence it gives to be one the reader applies whole. Once a search is open the fold of the settings opens, and closes again when a sentence is next read, unless the person opened or used it. No new ranking is asked for, and results already on screen stay. The line for `other` in `unmet` is left out, because the one line has said it | Say it another way, or use the settings |
| Read in part | `unread` is not empty, and the words changed the search: an edit of them did, or the page took a thing it noticed in them | Over what Burro understood, in the one line that says what was left out of the search: "Some of your words" is named last in it. Opened, it says that Burro read only some of what was typed, so that the ranking leaves the rest out, and while the box still holds what was sent it holds a button, "Show in the box", which selects in the box the stretches the API names. Until the evening of 2026-09-26 it was a notice of its own, directly under the box. Results for what was read | Say the rest again in shorter sentences, or use the settings |
| What was read of words that are no plain list | `suggestions` is not empty. The reader applies a prompt only when the whole of it is a plain list, and otherwise the service applies nothing and returns what it noticed as offers, each with the ways it may be taken | **Nothing is offered, and nothing waits on a press.** As soon as the reading is in, the page takes one way of every offer, sends the edits of every way it took in one request, in the order the things were noticed, and ranks. The results stand where Burro hopped. Each thing that was taken is a chip of what Burro understood, and what a person did not say in so many words says "assumed". Which way is taken is in the table under this one. What was taken of nothing is named in one line, under the line that says what happened and over what Burro understood, since it changes what the ranking means: a note headed "Left out of your search", which asks nothing and offers nothing (`LeftOut`). It names each thing by the name the service gives it, or by the page's own name for a budget or a journey where the service's holds a figure that was typed. It opens to why, in the browser's own element: of what counts recorded crime, the one account of when recorded crime counts; of what counts who lived somewhere, that Burro counts it only where a person chooses it; of a place Burro does not know, that it could not add the journey; of a thing the service gave no way to take, the service's own words. Each says that the thing is added under "Refine search". Drawn in full these stood over the answer, and after a sentence of a dozen things the first result began out of sight. Where nothing is ranked the line stands open, since there is no answer for it to give way to. The service's notice is shown as it came, but for its last sentence where that says that nothing of the words changed the search, which is no longer so once the page has taken what was noticed in them (`noticeOf` in `src/lib/search/said.ts`). Where a model reads too, what the rules read is taken and ranked first, and the page says that Burro is still reading the rest. What the model read is taken when it comes, and the ranking is worked out again: a thing that was taken as the rules read it is not taken a second time as the model did, or a wish would count for twice as much (`thingOf`). No box is headed "Choose what to add", no offer is drawn in four parts, no button adds several things, nothing is taken back at one press, and no fold holds what is left. Section 3 kept what each of those settled, under "What a browser showed" | Take a chip off, turn it, or change how much it counts. Add what was left out under "Refine search" |
| The language model would not read the words | `model_refused` is true, and `degraded` with it | "The language model would not read this. Burro's rules have read it instead." It stands where the line for a degraded reading would. The rules read the words as they would with no model, so their results show as usual | Use every control |
| Degraded to a form | `degraded` is true, or route 1 timed out, answered 5xx, or answered what is not the API's | "Your words could not be read just now. The settings do the same job." The fold of the settings opens, holding the returned spec, or `defaults` if none came back. Before a search the settings stand in the panel of Deep search, and the line names it in the place of the settings: a person can try again, or choose Deep search. If the rules read the words, their results show as usual. Where the words were not read, the line goes once a control is used | Use every control. "Try again" is beside the line for as long as it is there, and sends the same text |
| Burro not reached | A request for route 1 could not leave, or the website holds no address for the API | The error block: "Burro could not be reached." It does not say the words could not be read, and the settings are not opened: they could not reach it either. **Since 2026-09-27 it stands in the box, directly under the field, and is brought into sight**, as far as it must be: seen on a phone, a person pressed Search and saw the page as it had been, with what said that Burro could not be reached under the foot of the window. The field is left where it stood as Search was pressed, though the heading comes back over the box as the search closes. With results in sight nothing is moved | "Try again" sends the same text. "Start again" |
| Neutral notice | `notice` is `neutral_places` | `notice_text`, word for word, in a plain block with `role="status"` above the chips. Results for the rest. Nothing says what was left out. The API chooses between two sentences, by whether any edit changed the search, and the website adds no line of its own (section 13, gap 21, met) | Carry on. The block goes when the next sentence is sent |
| Error from the API | An `ErrorEnvelope` | 422 `invalid_text`: `message` under the box. 422 `unknown_place`, `unknown_area` or `invalid_spec` with `fields`: "Your search names something this data no longer has", and a button that sends the `remove` or `clear` for each path. Any other refusal of a sentence, a 404 for a wrong address of the API among them: `message` and the `X-Request-Id` in the error block, and never silence. 5xx: the last results stay, marked "not updated", with `message` and the `X-Request-Id` to quote | Try again, or start again from `defaults` |
| Offline | The browser says so, or a request cannot leave | "You are offline. Your search is still here." The last results stay readable. The latest edit waits | Read. The edit is sent once when the browser is back |

**Which way of an offer the page takes.** The page builds no edit of its own. Every edit it sends is one the service gave with a way of the offer, unchanged but for the place of a journey that held none. It chooses among the ways, in this order, and `src/lib/search/takes.ts` is the one place that does ([ADR 0012](../adr/0012-a-closed-vocabulary-and-a-guard-on-the-model.md), as amended, has why, and what it risks).

| # | The offer | The way that is taken | What its chip says was assumed |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The service names the way that one press may take (`add_all`): a wish at a mention, that a person rents or buys, the kind of home, a budget as it was worded, a journey as a guide | That way | Nothing, where it is the way the words give or the one way there is. Whether the limit is firm, where the words give another way |
| 2 | It names none, and the offer is of a limit that may be firm or a guide | The guide, whichever the words give: no area is left out on a guess | Whether the limit is firm, and that a person rents or buys where the edit says which |
| 3 | It names none, and marks one way as Burro's guess | The way that is the guess | How much the thing counts |
| 4 | A thing that runs one way, with no guess | More of what the name of the thing says | How much it counts |
| 5 | A journey to a place whose name several places bear | To the first of them the service gives | The place |

**A journey is taken as a guide whatever its words.** "At most", "max", "no more than" and "within" make minutes firm. Of a sentence that is no plain list the service offers such a journey both ways, marks the firm limit as its guess, and names the guide as what one press may take: the page takes the guide, by the first row, and the chip says "flexible assumed". On a build of London a journey is an estimate, worked out from distance with no timetable behind it ([ADR 0027](../adr/0027-a-journey-is-estimated-from-distance-until-a-timetable-is-held.md)), and no area is left out on an estimate without a press. A person makes the journey firm with a press of its own, in its chip or in the settings, and the count of areas ranked, the map and the table of all areas then say what that left out. The founder decided it on 2026-09-25 ([ADR 0012](../adr/0012-a-closed-vocabulary-and-a-guard-on-the-model.md), "What one press may take"). It was built both ways on 2026-09-27, and one line chooses: `A_LIMIT_THE_WORDS_MAKE_FIRM`, which is on `guide`. On `as_worded` such a journey is taken before any row of the table, by the way the service marks as its guess, so that the firm limit leaves areas out before anybody has pressed, and its chip says "firm limit". Where the service marks no way of a journey, the guide is taken whichever is chosen. **The line reaches a journey that is offered, and no other.** A plain list is applied by the service, as it is worded. And a journey to a name that several places bear is asked about by the service, in its own edit and as it was worded, of a sentence that is no plain list too: the page puts the first of the places into that edit and changes nothing else of it, so the journey is a firm limit where its words make it one. Measured on 2026-09-27, at both sizes, after "I think leafy, and within 25 minutes of Pellam": the chip says "Pellam Cross assumed, public transport assumed, 25 minutes, firm limit", 15 areas are ranked, and the table of all areas says of 7 "A journey is longer than a firm limit". After "I think leafy, and 25 minutes to Pellam" the chip says "flexible assumed", 21 areas are ranked, and none is left out for the journey. So areas are left out for a journey to a place that Burro chose, on a limit nobody pressed. It was so before the line was built, and is the founder's to decide (section 9, where the website is known to fall short). Section 9, check 62, has what was measured of each way.

To skip a thing is no way of taking it, and is never chosen. Nor is to stop counting it, but in one case, since 2026-09-27: where all that the service offers of a thing is to stop counting it, and it marks that as the way the words give, the thing is counted no longer, and its chip says that it is off. "Honestly, no station" turns away a thing that counts a little in every search. One line leaves it counting, as it was: `WHAT_THE_WORDS_TURN_AWAY`.

**Words that are read more ways than one count once**, since 2026-09-27. "Affluent" is read as a mix of brands, as what homes sell for and as the share of homes in the higher council tax bands. One reading is taken, which is the one the service marks as its guess, or else the first it gives of those that may be taken, and its chip says that it was assumed. The others are named in the line of what was left out, which says that the words were taken another way. Until then every reading was taken, and a person who wrote "slightly affluent" had three things ranked on. One line has every reading taken, or none: `OF_A_WORD_READ_SEVERAL_WAYS`.

**Where the words give no way, two kinds of offer are taken neither way**, since the night of 2026-09-26. The words give no way where the service names none and marks none as its guess. Until then more was taken of a thing that runs two ways, and the first way of a rule for an area: seen in a browser, the website ranked one area of 22 after "Leafy and quiet, near Gorsebeck", under a chip that said "Gorsebeck, only", and counted more pubs after "Pubs are so noisy".

| Taken neither way, where the words give no way | What the page does |
|---|---|
| A rule for an area: to look only there, or to leave it out. It is told by the edits the service gives with its ways, and by no name | It is left, and every area stays in: either way leaves areas out, and no area is left out on a guess. It is named among what was left out as what to do with the area, and opened it says how to say either. Since 2026-09-27 it is taken by the way the service marks as its guess, and by no other: that a way stands first, or may be added at a press, is no reading of the words. Where it is taken its chip says that it was assumed |
| A thing that runs two ways, more or fewer, or towards one end of a scale or the other | It is left, and named among what was left out: that Burro could not tell which way it was meant to count, and that it can be added under "Refine search". One line has more of it taken, as it was: `WHERE_BURRO_CANNOT_TELL` |

**What waits for a person is the service's to say, of every offer, since 2026-09-27** (`only_by_choice` and `by_name`, contract 8.2). Until then the website told what counts recorded crime and what counts who lived somewhere by what the service says each measure is of, and took neither, whatever the words were: so of a sentence that is no plain list, a person who typed "gritty", which is to ask for recorded crime by name, had it left out.

| Waits for a person | What the page does |
|---|---|
| A measure of recorded crime, or a vibe whose recipe holds one (`countsCrime`), **unless the service says that the person's own words name it** (`by_name`). To type "gritty" is to ask, and to type "posh" is not, though the same vibe is offered for both. A service that does not say has not said that the person asked. Which thing counts what is the service's to say, of every measure and of the recipe of every vibe, and no name of one is written into the website | Where it is named, it is taken as any wish is. Where it is not, it is left, and named among what was left out, which opens to the one account of when recorded crime counts (`ruleIn`): it counts only where a person asks for it by name, and a word that a vibe was offered for is no such asking |
| A measure that the service says is a fact about residents, or a vibe whose recipe holds one (`countsResidents`), whatever the service says of the offer: no word applies it, its own name among them | It is left, and named among what was left out, by the name the service gives the thing: that it counts who lived in an area at the census, that Burro counts that only where a person chooses it, and that it can be added under "Refine search" |
| Whatever else the service says waits for a person (`only_by_choice`): a measure that a decision holds to be offered and never applied, as the share of homes in the higher council tax bands is, and a wish or a journey that the words do not say is the person's own, as "my mum is after a park" | It is left, and named among what was left out, with why |
| A journey to a place Burro does not know, where the data holds none with a name like it | It is left, and named among what was left out |
| What the service gives no way to take | It is said in the service's own words |

A first answer that is held for the wait holds what was understood with it: what was read and what is ranked of it are shown together, where Burro hopped.

What building the search settled:

- **An edit is never lost to a call that is out.** Edits made while a call is out are kept in the store as `pending`. Each new edit is sent with the last spec the API returned and every edit still pending, in the order they were made, and the older call is stopped. The website still applies none of them. When an answer comes, `pending` is emptied. When a call fails, `pending` is kept and "Try again" sends it again.
- **A control moved while a sentence is being read waits for the reading.** Its edit goes with the spec the reading returns. If the reading fails or is stopped, the edit is sent with the spec as it was.
- **Nothing read and nothing changed are told apart.** Words that were read into edits that changed nothing, such as "renting" said to a renter, get "That changed nothing. Your search already says it." and not "could not be read". The line goes as soon as a control is used.
- **The settings can be ranked as they stand.** While there is no ranking, the settings hold a button that ranks by them, "Search with these requirements", which sends route 2 with the spec and no edit. It is how a person sees the defaults ranked without typing, and it is what ends a deep search. The button goes as it is pressed, the fold of the settings closes over the answer as the search opens, and the focus goes to what opens the fold.
- **Deep search makes a search by its button, and by nothing else.** The founder drew two ways in, and the second is built by choosing and then searching. What is chosen in the settings before a search is kept: both tabs stay, no result is drawn, and the map is as it is before a search, until "Search with these requirements" is pressed. Until the page was mended on 2026-09-26 the first setting that was moved opened a search: seen in a browser by three people, the tabs went and the page became a page of results under a person who had been told to choose and then to search, and by keyboard one press of an arrow key on a slider did it. It is built both ways, and `DEEP_SEARCH_RANKS_BY` in `SearchApp/look.ts` chooses. **Since 2026-09-27 the button is held at the foot of the window for as long as its own place is under it**, with what Burro says of what could not be sent over it. It stood at the foot of the settings, under ten groups of them: measured with the way chosen and nothing scrolled, 2,213 px down a desk's window of 900 and 2,641 down a phone's of 844, and a person who had chosen did not find it. It is at rest at the foot of the settings once the page is scrolled to it. `THE_BUTTON_OF_DEEP_SEARCH` in the same file has it held, or two of it, at the head and at the foot, or at the foot alone, as it stood. **"Nothing is ranked until the button" is so of the page, and not of the wire.** The website builds edits and never a search of its own, and only the service names a place and gives a journey its limits. So each thing that is chosen before a search is sent to the service to be applied: one `POST` to route 2 that asks for one area, whose ranking is thrown away, and no reasons are asked for. To choose renting, buying or visiting first sends nothing. Where the service cannot be reached it is said as a person chooses, with "Try again", and no search opens. No sentence of the website says that nothing is sent. Once a search is open a setting that is moved is ranked at once, whichever is chosen: a person is then refining an answer they can see. The settings of the second way in and the settings that refine a search are one part, in one place of the page.
- **Reasons must be for the ranking on screen.** Routes 2 and 3 each return a `spec_hash`, so the answer itself says which spec its reasons are for, and the website keeps no hash of its own making (section 13, gap 14, met). A hash is of the spec alone, and is the same on every release. So the release and the engine each answer names are kept with what the answer brought: the reading, the ranking, the reasons, each profile and each fact. A card shows reasons only when they are for the same spec as the ranking and the same release and engine made both. If they are not, as when the release moved between the two calls, route 3 is asked once more. Reasons that are still another release's are never shown: the cards say the reasons could not be loaded, and "Try again" ranks again.
- **What is on screen is of the release that made the ranking.** When a ranking comes from another release or engine than the one before it, the facts, the profiles and the reasons the other made go, and are asked for again. Reading the form again takes nothing away, whenever it comes: before this, a form that came after the reasons and the profiles emptied every card, and nothing asked for them again. A profile a browser answers from its own cache may be of the release before. It is shown, with the release that made it kept beside it, and is asked for again once when the ranking's release changes. A fact of the ranking's release is never given up for one of another.
- **The foot of the list names the release and the engine that made the ranking**, from the ranking's own answer, and not those the page was built on. It is said once, under the list, and not on every card.
- **A space requirement that is changed while no answer can come says so where it stands.** What says what failed stands in the box at the head of the page, with "Try again", and that there is no connection is said there too. A person who is among the space requirements does not see it: measured in a browser, the box stood 667 px over a window of 900 and 1,303 px over a window of 844, the control showed what it was set to, and nothing in sight said that the results did not. So once a search is open the page hands the space requirements the parts whose change waits (`partsOf` of what waits, in `src/lib/search/edits.ts`), and each control of such a part says one sentence under itself, where it says why an edit was refused: that the results do not show the change, and that it goes with the next change or with "Try again" (`NOTICE.unsent`), or once the connection is back (`NOTICE.unsentOffline`). A place that is being added is no journey of the search yet, and says it under the field that found it; how the journeys count says it under what sets it, and an area that is shown again under what shows it. It is said from the failure until the next answer, and while the change is tried again. The page is not moved to bring the failure into sight, which would move it under a press. What was pressed is held where it stood until the answer to it is drawn, and is let go as the asking ends where no answer comes (`SettingsPanel/held.ts`). Before a search what failed is said over the button of Deep search, which is held in sight, and no control says it.
- **Reasons or a profile that cannot be loaded are never met with silence.** The failure is kept whole, with the API's `message` and the `X-Request-Id`. The card says what it lacks, and the error block says why, with "Try again", which ranks again and asks for what is missing. Only the profile that failed is asked for again. It does not say the results were not updated, because they were. If the browser was offline, the offline line says so, and they are asked for again when it is back.
- **Stop puts the search back.** Reading a sentence replaces the spec before its ranking is in, so that the chips can be drawn at once. The search as it was when the sentence was sent is kept until that ranking comes. Stop puts it back, so the chips never say one search over the ranking of another. A sentence sent before the last one was ranked is undone with it. An edit made meanwhile is not undone: it is sent with the spec that was put back. Reasons that came for the ranking that was stopped are let go. **Since 2026-09-27 the line under the box says that the search was stopped**, and what became of the words, and it is heard as it is written. Of a first search: "Burro stopped, as you asked. No areas are ranked, and your words are still in the box." Over a search that is open: "Burro stopped, as you asked. Your search is as it was, and your words are in the box." It says so from the press until the search next moves, which is until a sentence is read or a ranking is asked for, an answer comes, or one fails (`wasStopped` in `src/lib/search/state.ts`). Until then the line said nothing of the press: after a first search it was empty, and whoever could not see the page could not tell that the press had landed. Seen so at both sizes on 2026-09-27, with the focus on Search after a first search and on "Start again" over one that is open.
- **One failure is shown in one place.** A refusal of the text is under the box. A reading that failed is the "could not be read" line, with "Try again". A reading that never reached Burro is the error block. Offline is the offline line. Anything else is the error block.
- **The line that says what was ranked names the first result the page has a name for.** An area the page was not built with has none. With a name for none, the line gives the count alone.
- **An answer that lacks what the contract requires is not read.** The client checks that the `data` of an answer holds every field the contract requires of that route, by name, from a list generated from the contract (`src/lib/api/required.ts`). One that does not is `unreadable`, and is said as any failure is. An answer that cannot be read and is no answer of Burro's, as what stands before Burro may send, is said by its status: of 429 that Burro is busy, and to wait a minute before trying again, and of 500 or more that Burro has a fault (`wordsFor`). Said to be an answer the page could not understand, it sent a person to try again at once. Before this, an answer with nothing in it left the page waiting for ever. A field or a code the website does not know is let through and breaks nothing: a line it has no words for is left out.
- **A ranking that fails leaves the list the reasons it has.** The reasons for a sentence are asked for beside its ranking, and may come first. Reasons that are for a ranking that has not come wait in the store, as `explanationsAhead`, while the reasons on screen stay. When that ranking comes they are its reasons. If it fails, the ranking on screen keeps its own. Found while building the comparison: before this, a second sentence whose ranking failed left every card waiting for reasons that never came.

What a browser showed, which no test had. **Since the evening of 2026-09-26 the page asks nothing**, so what is said below of an offer, of a question, of the one button that added several things and of the fold of what was left is of the page as it stood until then. Each is kept for what it found, which the page that asks nothing must still hold: that the answer is in sight after a press, that a firm budget is never taken in silence, that a journey is never a firm limit on an estimate, and that nothing moves because the focus came or went.

- **A part that was not read is said beside the box, and shown in the box.** A sentence of three parts gave one chip, and 21 areas were ranked as if all three had been read. The one sign was a grey line under the chips, off the screen, that did not say which part. Route 1 now says which stretches of the text it made nothing of, as offsets (`unread`), and the website works none of it out: `unread.ts` is deleted (section 13, gap 19, met). The box selects each stretch: the words stay in the box, and are written nowhere else on the page. With two stretches or more, each press selects the next. `src/lib/search/spans.ts` turns the API's offsets, which count code points of the text as sent, into places in the box. Where the words stand is known for the text that was sent, so the button goes as soon as the box is changed. That the ranking left something out is true of the ranking, and stays said until another sentence is read.
- **A budget that goes is said to have gone.** A change of tenure takes the budget off, because a rent is not a price (contract 5.3, rule 5). In a browser £1,700 a month vanished without a word when the next sentence said "Buying". The line that says what happened now says that the budget was taken off, and why.
- **How many areas changed place counts the areas that moved.** When a list of 20 became a list of 15 the page said "21 areas changed place". An area that went, or came, moves no other. The line says how many went or came, and then how many of the rest stand elsewhere than they did: "15 areas ranked, 6 fewer than before. 2 of the rest changed place."
- **A press lands where it was aimed.** With the focus in it the box was three lines high, by a rule of its style sheet. A press on anything under the box took the focus, the box dropped to one line, and everything under it jumped up by a line and a half between the button going down and coming up. The first press after every typed search was lost: a suggestion, a chip, the name of a result. How high the box is now follows what was done and never the focus alone (`data-tall`), and no rule of any style sheet that holds only with the focus, under the pointer or under a press may move or resize anything: `test/styles.test.ts` reads every one. The skip links of the search page were held to the same rule, and are laid over the start of what holds them where they once took a line of the page.
- **The page says that Burro is still reading only while something reads.** Search was pressed a second time, with the box as it was, while the model read. The first reading is stopped then, and the second call could not leave: the page said that Burro could not be reached, and went on saying that Burro was still reading the rest of the words. The store is now told when a reading is stopped, as it is when one fails, whatever becomes of the search that stopped it.
- **One press takes what was plainly said of the home, and says what it did.** The founder typed a sentence of their own that ended "If I'm buying, max £400k for a 1 bed flat". Thirteen things were offered, five were in sight, and the one button took three: quiet streets, a park and the journey. After the press the search still assumed renting and held no budget, and the answer took ten presses. Since 2026-09-25 the API marks what is plainly said of a home as Burro's guess and names it in `add_all` (contract, section 8.2), so the button takes it with the rest and its words count it. What carries a guess stands first among what is in sight. The line after the press says how many areas a firm budget left out, from `filtered` of the ranking that followed, and that the table of all areas lists each: a budget that leaves areas out is never taken in silence. Later that day the API came to offer a journey that was plainly said both ways by the rules alone, as it does where a model read it, so with no model on the journey is in sight and the button takes it as a guide. The website works none of it out: which way is taken, which is the guess and which areas were left out are each the API's.
- **After one press the answer is in sight.** The founder typed their own sentence on a build of a real city and pressed "Add the 6 that need no choice". The page said what was added, and then listed seven more offers, each several lines long, under a notice of what the data does not hold that was a fifth of the screen. The name of the first result was 1,626 px down a desk's screen of 900 and 2,149 px down a phone's of 844: a person pressed for an answer and had to scroll past seven questions to find one. Three things now give way to the answer. The offers that are left fold to one line, and open at a press: nothing is applied that was not pressed, and no offer is lost. What the data does not hold is said by name in one line, and why is one press away (section 3.1). And on a narrow screen the banner of a preview is one line once a search is open, as the banner of made-up data is. Section 4.1 gives what was measured. What Burro asks still comes before what it ranked without the answer: before any press the offers are in sight as they were.
- **Once the fold is opened and an offer is chosen, the answer is in sight again.** The fold was opened and one offer was added from it. The fold stayed open, with the seven that were left drawn whole, and the name of the first result was 1,925 px down a desk's screen of 900 and 2,569 px down a phone's of 844: the press changed the ranking, and the ranking was two screens away. What is left now folds again at once when one of them is added or skipped, and the line that opens it takes the focus, so that a person who wants to choose another presses once more where they are. It folds by the press and by nothing else. Two other ways were tried in a browser and put aside: to hold the fold open while the focus is inside it left the results out of sight for as long as a person chose, and moved the first result 1,232 px up the screen under the pointer as a press on it took the focus. To fold after a while with nothing pressed moved the page under a person who was reading. Nothing moves because the focus came or went: a press lands where it was aimed. The line says what was chosen since the press, and how many are left is on the line that opens them. Before any press a choice folds nothing, as before. Section 4.1 gives what was measured.
- **What is taken back is put back as the model has read it since.** The one button was pressed before the model had answered, and added what the rules said one press may add. The model then marked its guesses and gave two offers a way more. "Take it all back" put back what the rules had offered at the press, with no guess marked and without the ways the model had added. What one press added now keeps what the model has offered since, less what the person had chosen of before the press, and that is what is put back. Taken back before the model answers, it is what the rules offered, and the model's reading joins it when it comes.
- **Nothing is said to be unreadable while it is being read.** Where the rules noticed nothing and a model was asked, the page said at once that nothing in the words could be read, and under that that Burro was still reading the rest of them. It now says that Burro is still reading, and says that nothing could be read only once the model has read the words and made nothing of them either, or could not be asked.
- **What a press opens is seen.** What follows was seen of the page as it was until 2026-09-26, with three helpers under one box and the settings behind a button, and what it settled stands: the two helpers of Quick search and the fold of the settings are each brought into sight so. On a phone 844 px high the three helpers stood at the foot of the first screen, from 747, and what one opened began at 855, under the foot of the window: the helper turned amber, its arrow turned down, and nothing else was seen to happen. On a desktop its heading and one line were in sight. What a helper opens is now brought into sight once the press has landed, and only as far as it must be: until the whole of it is in sight, or until the helper stands at the top. What begins a quarter of the way up the window or higher is left where it is. **Since the page was mended on 2026-09-26 it is the head of what opened that is brought into sight**, which is 48 px of it (`HEAD` in `src/lib/sight.ts`), and nothing is moved where its head is in sight: a larger figure shows more of what opened, and moves the page further from under the hand. What was pressed has then gone from under the pointer, so for as long as a browser may count a press as the second of a double press, which is taken as half a second, such a press lands on nothing of what was opened: a press of its own lands at once, and so does a key. `src/lib/sight.ts` holds both. So is the table of all areas, which stands at the foot of the box of the map. Beside the answer that is a column that scrolls in itself, and at 1440 by 900 the table opened at 896, under the foot of the column, where on the trunk it opened in sight. The column is scrolled and the page is not: the head of the table is brought over the foot of the column. A fold does so where it is handed `bring`, as the panel that shares a search is, and the fold that says a search came from a link. So are the settings behind their button, which stands after the first result: on a phone it was pressed at 788 of 844 and the settings opened from 848, and they now stand whole, from 96 to 836, with their button over them. And so is the card of a word of the shelf, on a screen of any width. The shelf stands behind a helper, and once it is opened it stands at the foot of the window: at 1440 by 900 a word was pressed at 618 and its card opened from 862, so that the word turned amber, the map was coloured, and of the card the top edge was in sight. On a narrow screen the card was brought to the top of the window: on a phone a word was pressed at 653, and then stood 148 px over the top, out of sight, with the focus on it and a card of another name under it, since "lively" opens "Going out". The card was then brought up by its head, as far as left its word in sight over it. **Since 2026-09-27 what a hand opens stands where the whole of it has room**: under the line that holds what was pressed, or over it where there is room there and none under, and what was pressed stays where it was. The two helpers do so, and the card of a word of the shelf (`useWhereItOpens` in `Helpers/where.ts`). Where there is room neither way it stands under, and its head is brought into sight as it was. What the keys open stands under, where whoever goes by the order of the page finds it, and the whole of it is brought into sight. As what stood over a line closes, the page goes back by as much. One line has everything open under what was pressed, as it did: `WHAT_A_PRESS_OPENS` in `src/lib/sight.ts`. A fold, the table of all areas and the settings behind their button are brought into sight as they were. Section 9, checks 16 and 37, has what was measured.
- **Stop on a first sentence hands the focus to Search.** Over a search that is open Start again is drawn where Stop stood, and has the focus that Stop had. While a first sentence is read no search is open: Stop went with the press, nothing took its place, and the focus fell to the page as a whole, at both sizes, as it did before the look. The focus is now put on Search, which stands beside it and sends the words again.
- **The focus is never left on nothing.** It was, in four places. A chip that is removed gives the focus to the chip beside it. A question that was answered, or left out, gave it to what Burro understood, while the page asked. A pin that is pressed opens the card of its area by the map, and the card takes the focus: the map takes a press for its own, and the pin never took it. The pin says that it is expanded, and Escape closes the card. Closing the card of an area gives the focus back to the pin, or to the map. A button that may be pressed until it does nothing, as "Make the link", "Clear the budget" and the ends of a slider are, says that it is off (`aria-disabled`) and is never switched off: a button switched off while it has the focus loses it.
- **It was left on nothing in more places, which the look did not make and a walk of it found.** A way out of a search that no area passes, and a result whose area is hidden, give the focus to what Burro understood. "Try again", beside a failure or beside words that were not read, gives it to Search. "Start again" beside a failure gives it to the box. A cross of the bar of areas to compare gives it to the cross beside it, and the last cross to the bar itself. The bar held a way to clear it, "Clear", until 2026-09-26: each area is taken out by its own cross. "Try again" on a comparison that failed, and on a shared link that was not reached, leaves it where the answer will stand.
- **A second press on Stop does nothing.** Once a search has stopped, "Start again" is drawn where "Stop" stood. A finger that is not sure presses twice: the second press landed on "Start again", the box was emptied, and the whole search was gone, with all that was set by hand. "Start again" now does nothing for a press the browser counts as the second or a later one of a double press. A press of its own begins again as it did, and so does a key. Seen in Chrome with a mouse and with a finger, at both sizes, and in no other browser.
- **Under a press the face of a button steps into its shadow, and the button stays where it is.** It is the one thing that the rule above lets through. What takes the press is the button, which never moves and never changes size: what steps is the picture inside it, which gives way to the picture of the button pressed, of the same size and cut alike. Its words step with it, one art pixel down and one to the right, only where the system says that movement is welcome. `test/styles.test.ts` refuses a button that itself moves, a face that steps further than its shadow is wide, and a step under the pointer or the focus.
- **A box that holds a list laid over the page stands over what follows it.** A box of the look is a layer of its own, and one that comes later in the page is drawn over one that comes before. The field that finds a place lays its list over what is under it: on a phone the map was drawn over the third place of the list. The search box and what a helper opens are raised. So were the question of which place was meant, and the box of what Burro asked while a list of places was open in it.
- **A word of the shelf is as wide pressed as it was.** The open word was drawn with an edge of 2 px where a closed one has 1, so it grew 2 px wider and pushed every word after it on its line 2 px to the right. It is drawn in amber, with the edge it had.
- **One cobalt button is in sight, the one that matters most at that moment.** At 1440 by 900 the first screen held two filled buttons before a search, Search and "Rank with these settings", and three while Burro asks. Search is the button of the page that matters most, and is cobalt. While Burro asks and one press may add two things or more, that button is the one in cobalt and Search is drawn plain: it is the button it was, and does what it did. "Rank with these settings" was cobalt where the settings were one press away, a screen and more from Search, and plain where they stood open beside the box. Since 2026-09-26 Search and the button that makes a search of the settings are never in sight together before a search: each stands in the panel of its own tab, and is the one cobalt button of its panel. Since that evening Burro asks nothing, so no press takes the place of Search: it is cobalt wherever it is drawn. In the bar of areas to compare the way to the comparison was cream, so that a page held one cobalt button with the bar in sight. Since comparing is a headline it is cobalt: a page with the bar in sight holds two cobalt buttons, the one that sends a search at its head and the one that compares at the foot of the screen, and never side by side. `WAY_TO_COMPARE` chooses. While the panel that shares a search is open the button that makes the link is cobalt too, so a page may hold three, of which the last two may be in sight together.
- **Once a search is open the line under the box always says something.** It keeps the room of a line, so that nothing moves when it speaks, and since 2026-09-27 the room of the most it has said since the search opened, at the width it was measured at: what it says of a first ranking stands in two lines beside the map, and what it says once a chip is turned or taken off stands in one. Where Burro asked, while it did, and where nothing was read, it said nothing, and its room was an empty band in the box, 72 px on a desktop and 44 on a phone. It now says that Burro has not ranked any areas yet, and "Working out the ranking" while a first ranking is on its way. Once a search is stopped it says so, and before a search too, where a first search was stopped.
- **A setting that is moved before a search stays where it stood in the window as the search opens.** The heading and the tabs over the settings go and the answer is drawn about them: seen on a phone, of the page as it was, the plus of a slider went from 501 px of 844 to 23, from under the hand. Two promises meet there, and both cannot be kept: that a press lands where it was aimed, and that the first result is in sight once a ranking follows a press. It is built both ways. As it is, the setting is held where it stood, and the answer is drawn over it and under it, out of sight, as it is whenever a setting is moved there with a search open. The other way brings the box into sight with the answer under it, and the setting goes from under the hand. `A_SETTING_OPENS` in `SearchApp.tsx` is the one line that chooses, and it is the founder's. Until 2026-09-26 it was so under 80rem alone, where the settings stood behind a helper: it was then so at every width, since the settings stand in the panel of Deep search. **It is met only where the look has a setting make the search** (`DEEP_SEARCH_RANKS_BY`): as chosen, the button makes it, the button goes with its press, and the focus goes to what opens the fold.
- **Where the settings are ranked while a search is open and nothing is ranked, the first result is brought into sight.** It was found while Burro asked: nothing was ranked then, so the settings held the button that ranks by them. It is so still where nothing of a sentence was read. A press on it drew the first result 1,882 px down the page, under the offers, as it was seen at 1440 by 900 where the settings stood open at the left. Once the ranking of that press is in, the page is moved to the first result, which then stood from 319 to 727 of 900. What Burro asks is left as it was, above it. The settings are in their fold since, and the press hands the focus to what opens it.

- **What a person set by hand is never set back by words the search has already read.** A person typed "Renting a 1 bed up to £1,700 a month, leafy and quiet, 35 minutes to Cindermoor Works" and searched. They set the budget to £1,800, the journey to 30 minutes and Leafy to 80 by hand, typed "a bit more green space, and ignore the high street" after the sentence in the box, which is labelled "Add to this search", and searched. All that the box held was sent, with the search as it stood. The service keeps nothing, and cannot tell words it has read from new ones, so it read the first sentence a second time, onto the search that had been set by hand: the budget was £1,700 again, the journey 35 minutes, Leafy 100, and Quiet streets, which nobody had touched, counted for 75 where it had counted for 50. Nothing said so. The search now keeps count of how far into the box it has read (`src/lib/search/mark.ts`), and the box sends what stands after that, as it was typed. The walk is recorded from the service, step by step (`record_what_was_added`), and `test/search/added.test.tsx` holds the website to it to the letter. What follows is what it settled.

| What a person does | What is sent | What the page says |
|---|---|---|
| Types after what the search has read, and searches | What was typed after it, with the search as it stands. It begins with the comma or the full stop it was typed after: the service reads it as it reads a sentence that begins with none | What it says of any sentence. Where all of it was read, nothing more: the first result is whole on the first screen of a phone with not a line to spare (section 4.1) |
| The same, where words of what was added were not read, or nothing of it was | The same | Under the box: "Burro read what you added by itself, without the words before it. Say the whole of a thing in what you add." Words that rest on what came before them, as "25 minutes max", "by bike" and "forget the budget" do, are not read by the rules when they are sent alone. Sent with the sentence before them they were not read either, and the sentence was offered again, thing by thing, over the search it had made |
| The same, where nothing came of what was added: no edit, no offer and no question | The same. The search has not read words that nothing came of: they are still to be read. Said another way where they stand, or with the words they rest on put before them, they are sent again, with what was changed and what was added. Nothing of them was made part of the search, so nothing of them can be read twice | "Nothing in that could be read", as of any sentence, and the line above |
| Presses Search with nothing new in the box | Nothing | Under the box, as a fault of the box is said: "There is nothing new in the box to read. Type more after what is there, or use the settings." The focus goes to the box. It sent the sentence again, and a sentence sent twice made what it names count for more. While a model still reads the words, the box says that in its place: "Burro is still reading the rest of your words." |
| Changes a word among those that were read, and searches | Nothing. Nothing holds the words as they were, so nobody can say what was changed to what, and to read the sentence again is to set back what was set by hand | "Burro reads what is added at the end of the box, and not a change to the words before it. Type the change at the end, or use the settings." |
| The same, and types more at the end | What was typed at the end | Once it is read: "Burro read what you added, and not your change to the words before it. Type the change at the end of the box, or use the settings." It is said once |
| Takes out the end of what was read, or types over it | What stands from where the change began: it is new | What it says of any sentence |
| Takes words that were read out of the box, as by selecting all of it and typing a letter or pasting over it, and puts them back with the keys that undo | Nothing. They are the words that were read. Where nothing but that was done to the box, the search knows of the box what it knew before they were taken out | "There is nothing new in the box to read. Type more after what is there, or use the settings.", as of any box that holds nothing new |
| The same, where more was done to the box between, or where words that stood in their place were read since | Nothing of what came back, and what is typed after it. The keys that undo say too little of what they did for it to be told from what is new: so as many characters after what was read as were ever taken out of it are taken as read | "Burro reads what is added at the end of the box, and not a change to the words before it." Once what is added is read: "Burro read what you added, and not your change to the words before it." |
| Presses the keys that undo, and then the keys that do again, once or many times in a row | What stood after the words that were read before the keys were pressed. Each press of the one set of keys leads back from a press of the other, and the search knows of the box what it knew then: what was pasted over all of the box, undone and done again, is sent, as none of it was read | What it says of any sentence |
| Undoes words that were read out of the box, or has those keys put other words where words that were read stood | Nothing of what they put there. What stands after it is sent | The same two lines: the words before what is added are not as they were read |
| Presses the keys that do again once the search has read more since the keys that undo were pressed | What stands after the words that were read | The same two lines: nobody knows what those keys put where |
| Types after what was read, undoes it with those keys and does it again | What was typed: it was never read, and nothing that was read was taken out of the box | What it says of any sentence |
| Copies, cuts or drags words that were read, or that are sent to be read after they were copied, and pastes them into the box: over themselves, where they stood, or after what is there | Nothing of them, and nothing that stands before them. What is pasted, and is as long as what was last taken from the box, is taken for the same words | "Burro reads what is added at the end of the box, and not a change to the words before it." Once what is added after them is read: "Burro read what you added, and not your change to the words before it." |
| Pastes words that come from somewhere else | What was pasted, as what is typed is sent: it is new | What it says of any sentence |
| Empties the box by hand, or types over all of it | All that the box then holds, with the search as it stands: it is added to the search | What it says of any sentence |
| Presses "Start again" beside the box | Nothing. The box is emptied, and the search that begins has read nothing | The page as it first stood |
| Presses "Start again" beside a failure | Nothing. The box is left as it is. The search that begins has read none of it, so Search sends all of it | The page as it first stood, with the box as it was |
| Presses an example | Nothing. It fills the box in place of what was there, so nothing in the box has been read. An example is offered only before a search | |
| Presses "Try again" or Search after words could not be sent, could not be read, or were stopped | The same words again, with whatever was typed after them since. Words are read once their answer is in and something came of them, and not before | What it says of any sentence |
| Comes back to the search from another page | Nothing. The box is drawn again and is empty: what was typed was nowhere else. So nothing in it has been read, and what is typed in it is sent whole | The search as it was |

  - **Where words stand is counted from the start of what was sent.** The service counts where the words of an offer stand, and which stretches were not read, from the start of the text it was sent, in code points. The store keeps where that text began in the box, as a count, until the box changes. The box hands the page the words from there, and selects among them counted from there, so "You wrote", "Show the words" and "Show in the box" fall on the words they are of, whatever stands before them and whatever characters it holds.
  - **What is selected is brought into sight.** A browser brings the caret into sight when a box takes the focus, and what is selected not at all. Words that stand after a sentence are under the three lines that the box shows, and were selected where nobody saw them: "Show in the box" said that the part was selected, over a box that showed its first three lines. The box now puts the caret at the end of the words, takes the focus, and selects back from there.
  - **The most the box takes is of what is added.** The box holds what the search has read as well as what is typed after it. The API takes so many characters at a time, so the count of characters left, and the most the box takes, are of what would be sent. A browser cuts what is pasted or dropped at what the box takes, and says nothing of it: 900 characters were pasted, the box held 600 that ended in the middle of a word, and the search read them. So where what was pasted fills the box, the box says under itself how much Burro reads at a time, and that the end was left out if what was pasted was longer (`PROMPT.full`). It says that it may have been, and never that it was: the website does not read what was pasted, only how much the box then holds.
  - **An offer that was not chosen goes when the box changes, as it did, and the next reading does not bring it back.** It came back because the words it rested on were read again. To be offered a thing again, a person types it again.
  - **A change that cannot be placed leaves nothing to be read a second time.** Where the box was changed is worked out from where the caret stood and where it is left, as counts. Where they do not add up, or the box holds more or less than it last said, as when a tool that fills boxes in has changed it, the change may have been made anywhere: all that the box holds is taken as read, and as changed, so that no word is read twice. A person then types what they want at the end.
  - **Words that were read are not new when the keys that undo put them back.** Seen in a browser on 2026-09-26, against the service, on the search that had been set by hand: all of the box was selected and a letter typed over it, by accident, and the keys that undo put the sentence back. The letter had left nothing in the box that was read, so the sentence came back after the place the search had read to, and Search sent all of it. The budget was £1,700 again, the journey 35 minutes, Leafy 100 and Quiet streets 75, and nothing said so. With the end of the sentence taken out and put back, "35 minutes to Cindermoor Works" was sent, and the journey was 35 minutes again. With more typed after the sentence, read, undone and done again, it was sent twice, and what it named counted for more. A browser says that a change was undone or done again, and never what the change was. So the search keeps count of how many characters that were read were taken out of the box (`gone`), and what it knew of the box at the times those keys lead back to (`back` for the keys that undo, `again` for the keys that do again): before the first of the words that were read was taken out, with how many characters have come into the box since, and before each press of either set of keys. Where the keys leave the box holding as many characters as it held then, and the keys that undo took out as many as had come, it holds what it held, and the search knows of it what it knew. All of it is let go once anything else is done to the box, and once the search moves on. Otherwise what those keys put back stands where what was read ended, and there is no more of it than was ever taken out: so many characters after that place are taken as read, and the box says that words before what is added were not read. It costs a person who took out words that were read, typed more, and undid a part of what they typed: of what they typed, as many characters as they took out may go unread, and the box says so. What is typed after words that were read, undone and done again, is new as it was: nothing that was read was taken out.
  - **Where those keys lead is known by what they leave selected, and said where it is not known.** What was kept was first put back whenever the keys that undo left the box as long as it had been, and they may lead further back than that, to a box as long that holds other words. That was found by changing many boxes at random and following every word, as a test does, and not in a browser: it needs what was typed before a search and after it to be undone as one, and in Chrome a search that was pressed with Enter was seen to part them. So the keys that undo are told from the keys that do again, and each leads back only from the other, or from words taken out. Chrome leaves what the keys that undo put back selected, which was seen for a paste, for words typed over and for a letter rubbed out, and leaves a caret after what the keys that do again put back. What is selected says where what came back stands, where the counts alone would say that characters came at the end: a word typed over another before it was sent, and put back by those keys once the other was read, is as long a change as none at all. Where those keys take out words that were read, what they put in their place is taken with the words before the place, and that those are not as they were read is said. So it is where the keys that do again are pressed once the search has read more. A browser that leaves no more than a caret after the keys that undo says less. No such browser was driven: no word that was read is sent again in one, by the same walks at random, and words that those keys put in place of others may go unread there with nothing said.
  - **Nor are they new when a copy of them is pasted.** Seen in the same walk: all of the box was selected, copied and pasted over itself, which left the box as it was, and Search sent all of it, onto the search that had been set by hand. So with a copy pasted after the sentence, and with the sentence cut out and pasted back. Words cut from the middle of the sentence and pasted at its end were read again too, and Leafy, which had been set to 80, was 100. The box keeps where what was last copied, cut or dragged from it began, how many characters it was, and whether any of it had been read: counts, held by the box itself while it is drawn, and never in the store. What is then pasted into the box, and is as long, is those words: it is taken as read, and what stands before it with it, wherever it is put. It is told from what was read by where it was taken from, then or since: a sentence that is copied before it is sent is read once it is sent. That it was read is kept from then on, whatever is done to the box: seen in a browser on 2026-09-26, a sentence was copied, sent and read, and three things set by hand. All of the box was typed over with "Near a park. " and the sentence pasted after that. Nothing in the box was read by then, so the place it was copied from said nothing, and all of the box was sent: the budget was £1,700 again, the journey 35 minutes and Leafy 100. So what was copied from the box counts as read from the moment words of the box are sent. It costs a person who copied words, took them out before anything was sent, and pastes them later: they are taken as read, and the box says so. What is copied somewhere else on the page takes the place of what was copied from the box, and a search that begins again has read nothing of it. The clipboard holds the words, and the website never reads it. So what is copied outside the page is not heard of: words of the same length, copied elsewhere after words of the box were, are taken for the words of the box when they are pasted, and the box says that words were not read.
  - **Typing what is added draws nothing again.** What is typed after the words that were read moves no count that the store holds, so the store tells nobody of it, and the page is not drawn again for each letter.

### 3.1 What the data does not hold

A release may hold no journey, no cost and too little of a recipe to place any area on a vibe. A preview of real data does. The page then says what is not there, in plain words, and offers nothing it cannot answer. Nothing is filled in.

| Where | What it does | Reads |
|---|---|---|
