| Under the box, before a search | The hint says what can be asked for: with no cost and no journey it asks only for what a person would like nearby, and says that Burro has no rents, prices or journey times for these areas yet | `holds` of route 11 |
| The helpers | A helper the release gives nothing to offer is not drawn: "Try an example" where it can answer the whole of no example, "Start from a word" where no vibe places any area | `holds`, `recipes`, `features` |
| The shelf | The words of the vibes that place some area come first. The rest stand under "Not in this data yet", behind "More words". The card of such a word has no "Add to my search" and no map: it says that no area can be placed on it, how much of its recipe is held as one number, and what it waits on, by name | `recipes` of route 11 |
| A place you need to reach | With no place in the data there is no field: the label stays, and a line says that nothing typed there could match | `holds.journeys` |
| Examples | Three sentences, chosen from a list of six in order: the first three the release can answer the whole of. With nothing it can answer, none is drawn | `holds`, `recipes`, `features` |
| Under the box, after a search | "Not in this data yet": each thing that was asked for and is in the data for no area, by name, in one line. It opens, by the browser's own element, to why, and for a vibe to what it waits on: drawn in full it was 192 px high on a desk and 288 on a phone, above the answer. It stands directly under the status line, before the chips. It is drawn as a notice, with the arrow of the look before its words, and each thing it opens to has its drawing beside its name, in outline, as a thing that counts for nothing has. The line is 40 px high on a phone | `not_in_release` of route 1, and an edit refused as `not_in_release` |
| The settings | A budget keeps the kind of home and says that the data holds no cost. Journeys say that the data names no place. A vibe no area is placed on has no slider | `holds`, `recipes` |
| A result | The result names in sight what was asked for and has no figure: a line for the thing, by its name, which says "no data", and beside the fit "approx data", after the mark of what is not whole. **Under its fit it says that the area is listed lower for it**, in one sentence: "This area is listed lower because it has no data for something you asked for." Seen in a browser on 2026-09-27, after a sentence of seven things: fits of 54, 44, 35, 53 and 34, and nothing that said why 53 stood under 35. That the person asked for it, and what nobody chose and has no figure, are said under the working. Until 2026-09-27 all of it stood on the result, under what the fit rests on, with the mark of a trade-off before what was asked for. The order of the list is the engine's, and is not changed | `contributions[].present`, the provenance of each weight |
| The map | The legend explains a pattern only where the map draws it. A vibe no area is placed on colours no map | `filtered`, `unranked`, `recipes` |
| The page of vibes | A vibe no area is placed on has no map and names no area. Every vibe says how much of its recipe is held. An end that holds more than twelve areas says how many, and names them one press away | `recipes`, the bands of route 4 |
| The page of an area | "Vibes Burro could not work out" tells a vibe that no area can be placed on from one that cannot place this area alone. What homes cost says what a range means only where there is a range. Where the data names no station for any area, the page says that of the data, once, where the nearest station would stand: that Burro has no information about stations yet. It does not say that no station is near the area, which a vibe of the same page may contradict with the distance to one. The part that says where to start is then left out | `recipes`, the facts of route 6, `counts.stations` of route 11 |
| Methods | A measure no search ranks on alone says which vibes it is a part of. With no journey and no cost, the limits of neither are given, and how each works is said as what is to come | `holds`, `recipes`, `tags` |
| Sources | A credit that holds a gap the publisher's words leave to be filled, such as "[year]", is shown as it came, and is said to be unfinished. Nothing is written into the gap | `attributions` |
| Sources | What the terms of a publisher ask to be said wherever its credit is shown stands under the credit, in the API's words, and under no other source. Beside a figure it follows the credit, wherever the credit stands there | `said_with_attribution` of `attributions` and of a fact's `sources` |

**Where an area is** is two small pictures: the whole of the data as one shape, with a ring where the area stands, and the area drawn close among those around it. It was one picture of every area, and on a thousand areas the area could not be found in it.

**The outlines of a page of maps are drawn once.** The page of vibes draws the outline of each area once, and each map points at it. A pointer says its band and where its outline is, and nothing else: `<use data-band="3" href="#o12">`. Whose the outline is, the page says once, on the outline. Measured on 2026-09-25 on a build of a thousand areas and fourteen vibes: the page was 13,955,218 bytes as it was built, of which 26,052 pointers were 7 million, each written with a class, the id of its area, and the id again in where it pointed, in what is drawn and again in what React is sent to take the page up. It is 8,924,171 bytes, and 1,250,463 as it travels, where it was 1,465,203. The areas are drawn in the order they were, so that the page is the same to the point of the screen: two pictures of the whole page, before and after, differ in no point at either size. What is left is the 13,326 links of the page, which are 3.5 million bytes, and what React is sent, which is two thirds of the page. The outlines a build asks the service for are kept for as long as a built page is, so that a build of a thousand pages asks once for each process that builds.

**A read that a busy service was late with is asked for again.** A build of a thousand pages asks one service from every worker at once. An area alone is answered in a tenth of a second, and behind a hundred others it is not answered in ten, and the one late answer stopped the whole build. A read made while building that timed out, brought no answer, or met a service that said it was busy (429, 503 or 504) is now asked for four times at most: it is waited for 10 seconds, then 20, 40 and 80, and the service is left alone for 1, 2 and 4 seconds between. What the API refused is an answer, and is asked for once. If the last try fails the build stops, and says which read it was, why, and after how many tries. The framework itself gives a page 60 seconds and then begins it again, so the longest waits are never used up: a page whose reads have taken that long is begun anew, and its reads with it. Measured against the service on a build of a thousand areas, sixteen areas asked for at once were answered in 1.8 seconds, one after another. Fourteen workers ask for 112 at once, which is some 13 seconds: more than the first wait, and less than the second. Only a build waits longer: a person's browser waits as long as it did (`src/lib/api/server.ts`, and `server.test.ts` holds each of these).

## 4. The result card

The answer comes first. The first five results are cards, because route 3 explains five at most. A card is the answer in short, and opens to its working in place. Results 6 to 10 are rows, and "Show 10 more" shows rows 11 to 20. Under a list of more than five one line says that Burro writes out why an area fits for the first five results only, and that it is read by pressing "Show the working" on any of them: since 2026-09-27 it says where, because no result says why it fits. Under it the list names the release and the engine that ranked the areas.

**The founder walked the website a third time, late on 2026-09-26, and asked for less on a result**: "Again, we don't need to over complicate and bloat with too much info." Until 2026-09-27 a result held how much its fit rests on, a line for each vibe with where the area sits in words, one reason under "Why it fits", and the trade-off, each sentence with the key of its source. **It holds its heading, a line for each thing that was asked for, its trade-off and two ways on, and no more.** Every reason and every key of a source is under its working, one press away ([ADR 0046](../adr/0046-a-result-shows-what-was-asked-for-as-a-name-and-a-gauge-and-the-source-of-every-figure-stands-under-its-working.md)). This section says what a result is since. What stood before is said where it says why a thing is as it is, and the record has what was given up.

**That areas can be compared is said with the list**, before anybody has chosen one: in a sentence, with two small drawings of the look beside it, of a house and of a block of flats, a person is told that they may choose two to four areas and that Burro then sets them side by side. It is said by the search page, beside what refines the search and on its line, and by the list under its first result where a result is narrow, so that nothing more stands between the box and the answer (`Invite`). As the page was first rebuilt it stood over the list, and with the fold of the settings and the line in whole sentences it put the first result of a desk at the very foot of the window (section 4.1). It names the button as the button names itself ([ADR 0039](../adr/0039-comparing-areas-is-a-headline-of-the-website-and-nobody-wins-a-comparison.md)).

**How a result is drawn.**

| Part | Drawn as |
|---|---|
| A card | A box of the look. It is laid out by its own width and not the screen's, so that it holds in the middle column of a wide screen. Under 30rem of its own width its parts stand 4 px apart and are set at 15 px, and the lines of its trade-off stand at 1.4 of their size where a page of reading stands at 1.5, which is what lets the first result be whole on the first screen of a phone |
| A row, results six and after | Within a plain edge of ink, with no shadow. Rows that stand together share an edge. Ten boxes with shadows, one under the other, are noise |
| The rank | A pennant with its number set on it in type, in the reading face. The pennant of the first is the pennant of the tenth |
| The name | In the face of names, at 30 px. What stands beside the name stands under it |
| The fit | In the reading face, heavy, because it is a figure and is read. As large as the name where the card has room |
| The town of the area | At the far end of the heading, after the name and the fit, on a card and on a row alike: 112 by 64, the first as the tenth. It is drawn from where the page holds that the area sits, which the search page and the page a shared link opens are built with: so it is the town of the page of its area, whatever was searched for. What it leaves blank, which is what is not known of the area, is said under the heading. **The town of a result stands without its line.** A line said what a town is drawn from, and that it is no picture of the place: "Each town is drawn from four of its area's vibes. Not a picture of the place.", as it was first written. It stood once over the list, in a slip of cream, and then under the heading of the first result. The founder walked the website a second time, and asked for it to go from a result: `LINE_STANDS` in `ResultList/look.ts` is `none`, and the line is put back in that one line. It went from the bar of areas and from the head of a comparison with it, which said the same of the same towns (`BAR_SAYS_OF_ITS_TOWNS`, `HEADS_SAY_OF_THEIR_TOWNS`). What a town is, and that it is no picture of the place, is said in the key to the drawings and at the head of the page of an area. A result under 36rem of its own width has no town: so no result of a phone has one ([ADR 0034](../adr/0034-the-town-of-an-area-is-drawn-by-rule-and-is-no-picture-of-the-place.md)) |
| A line of a result | One line for each thing that was asked for, on a rule of sand, in three columns: the small drawing of the thing with its name; its gauge, which is five steps with a peg on the one the area sits on, and one picture at each end; and, where the figure is not whole, the mark of what is not whole with the two words "approx data". **A vibe and a measure have a gauge. A journey and the budget have a figure where a gauge would stand**, in the reading face, and after it one word, the heavier of the two, which says on which side of what the person set the figure falls: "within" or "over". Neither word is coloured, and neither side is marked as the better. Every line is as high as the line of a gauge, whatever it holds. **The gauge of a measure has the blank picture at both ends**, since none is drawn of a measure or of a family of measures, and runs towards what the measure is called (`A_MEASURE_RUNS`). **No words say where the area sits.** Until 2026-09-27 the band stood in plain words after the steps, as "around the middle", and under them what the band rests on: the founder asked for both to go. A step is chequered where the band rests on part of what its vibe needs, as it was, and the mark beside the two words is that step, alone. A mixed area is a rail over the steps it spans, and an area that cannot be placed is five empty steps and no peg, with the two words "no data". **Each end of every gauge has one picture**, whichever way its vibe runs: what each end is, of a vibe that runs between two ends that have names, and little of the thing and much of it, of a vibe that runs one way. It is chosen by the id of the vibe and by which end it is, and the blank one stands at both ends of a vibe that has no pair. Until 2026-09-27 an end of a scale was two pictures side by side, a vibe that runs one way had none, and on a narrow result the pictures gave way to the names of the ends. The names of the two ends are not drawn on a result: the pictures stand for them, and one line of the look names each end under its picture (`ENDS_ON_A_RESULT`). **The steps of every line of a result begin on one line down the result**, whatever the length of the name before them, and on the same line from one result to the next: the founder asked that a person can read down them. So the names before them have a column of one width, which is as wide as the longest name that is drawn needs, and no wider than about a quarter of the result: a name that is longer breaks onto a second line, and none is cut. A style sheet cannot be told how wide a name is, so it is told the names, which are the service's, and lays each in the column of every line, where none is drawn and none is heard (`Strip/column.ts`). The picture of an end is of one size whatever its vibe, so the room of an end is as wide on every line. Where a result has the room the two words stand after the gauge, and where it has not they stand under the name. **In a result narrower than a phone gives, under 21.2rem of its own width, the gauge has the line under its name** and the two words the line under the gauge, each as far in as the name stands, so that the steps begin in one place still: measured on 2026-09-27 in a window 320 px wide, a name and a gauge side by side wanted 303 px of a line of 268, the gauge ran 35 px past its result and the page scrolled sideways. A result in a phone 360 px wide has the room, and is as it was. Section 4.1 has what was measured |
| "Why it fits" | On no result, since 2026-09-27. It is a heading under the working, on a plate of ink, over every reason the service gives. Until then it stood on a result, on a board of hedge green, before the first reason |
| "Trade-off" | On a band of its own, from edge to edge of the result, over the two ways on: sand, between two rules of ink. Its heading stands on a plate of ink at the head of its sentence, with a pair of scales before the plate, whose pans hang level, and the sentence runs beside the heading and then under it. Every word of it is ink, and nothing of it is poppy: it stands out by where it stands and how it is drawn, and never by red, which says danger. Until 2026-09-27 it stood on a board of cream with a stepped mark of poppy, which the founder read as a red arrow. It is built a second way, as a box of its own within the result, cream within an edge of ink with the hard shadow of the look, with two arrows before its heading, one each way: `TRADE_OFF_DRAWN` in `ResultList/look.ts` chooses |
| The ways on | The way to compare stands in the heading of the result, by its town where it has one, and is the most visible of the three: "Add to compare", and once its area is chosen "Added to compare", in amber, which takes the area out again. **Under 46rem of its own width a result has no room for it beside its name and its fit, and it has a line of its own after them, at the far end**, at every width: until 2026-09-27 a result from 30rem to 36rem, which a window from 960 to 1120 px wide gives, drew it beside them, and a name was left 26 px and broken inside the word. On a narrow result the three are as high as a small button of the look, 34 px, so that the way to compare costs the first result of a phone no height. "Show the working" and "More like this" are buttons of the look, in cream. None is cobalt: Search is. "Show the working" is amber while its working is open. Until 2026-09-26 the way to compare was the third of three buttons at the foot of a result, named "Compare" |
| The working | A box of its own under the card, headed by a board of ink that says whose working it is, as "Farrowmere: the working". With several open a page is long, and each says whose it is |
| "Source" | A key and the word, on a rule. It opens in place to a box of cream within an edge of ink, every word of it in ink: the name of a source is a link, and a link is never read on sand. The edge was dashed until 2026-09-26: no edge of the website is. **Since 2026-09-27 it stands on no result as it is first shown**: under the working every sentence and every figure ends in it, as it did |
| A cost | The figure, and a bar drawn in pixels: a block of sand for the range, a post of ink for the middle, a taller post with a sign of amber for the budget |
| What holds the place of a sentence | A line of dashes in sand. It does not move |

Nothing on a result is a drawing of Burro, a medal, a star or a prize, and no result is drawn larger or in another colour for its rank. The town of a result is handed neither its rank nor its fit. No picture at an end of a gauge says that the end is the better one, and nothing on a result is red.

**In short, before anything is opened.**

| Part | Shows | Filled from | When there is none |
|---|---|---|---|
| Heading | Rank, name, what stands beside the name, and fit as "78 of 100". The name is a link to the page of the area: one press from a result to its portrait. Beside the name, smaller, is the label its publisher gives the area, which says its borough, and the words "draft name" where no person has checked the name. An area that bears no name but its label has its borough there, unless its name says it. A row says it as a card does. It left all of it out until 2026-09-25: seen in a browser, results 6 to 10 gave a name alone, which was taken for a name somebody had checked, and two areas of one name in two boroughs could not be told apart. The heading of a row has a line of its own, and the three ways on stand together on the next | `RankedArea.rank` and `.score`. `AreaSummary.name`, `.borough`, `.named` and `.slug`, joined on `area_id` | Nothing to rank by: no fit |
| That the fit is not whole, beside the fit | The mark of what is not whole, and the two words "approx data". They stand where the fit is based on some of what counts, where the journeys count for nothing in it because none of them has a time, and where a firm limit could not be held against the area. What the fit is based on, what it leaves out and each such limit are said in full under the working, after the same mark and the same two words. Until 2026-09-27 each was a sentence under the fit, as "This fit is based on 4 of the 5 things that count in your search." One line of the look says them there again (`WHAT_A_FIT_IS_BASED_ON_IS_SAID_IN_THE`) | The count of `contributions` with `present` true, over the count of all. `untested_filters`. The `commute` contribution with `present` false, and `legs` | All present: nothing, on a card as on a row |
| The lines | **One line for each thing that was asked for, in the order the search holds them: a vibe, a measure, a journey, the budget.** Until 2026-09-27 a result drew a line for each vibe that was asked for and for nothing else that was: a search for one journey drew two vibes that nobody asked for, and nothing of the journey. First one for each `StripMark` that was asked for, in the order the API gave them: the small drawing of the vibe with its name, and its gauge with a picture at each end. Then one for each vibe that was asked for and could not be worked out for the area, with a gauge of five empty steps and "no data". Then one for each measure that was asked for, by the short name the API gives it: on a card its gauge, by the band its fact holds among the areas Burro compared, and on a row its name and no more, since the ranking gives no band and the page holds the facts of the first five results alone. Then one for each journey, by the name of its place: how long it takes, in minutes, and on which side of its limit that falls, or of an estimate its band in the API's words, "approx data" and no minutes. Then one for the budget: what a home of the kind costs, which is what the budget was held against, and on which side of the budget that falls. A thing with no figure says "no data" on its line. A setting nobody chose has no line, and a visit has none of a budget. **The vibes that nobody asked for are under the working**, of which the API gives two at the most: they stand on a result only where nothing at all was asked for, so that a result is never without a line (`OTHERS_STAND`). Where both are drawn they stand in a list of their own after what was asked for, and "Asked for" is said once before the lines that were asked for and "Other vibes" before the others. **Each list is named for what it holds**, to whoever hears the page: "What you asked for in" the area, and "Vibes of" the area where nothing was. **A result as narrow as a phone shows three rows of lines where they take more than four, and one press under them shows the rest**, which says how many more there are: a line is one row and half a row more for each line more of its name, and every line that says "no data" stands over the press. The picture of the gauge of a measure says the figure and where it stands, in the API's words. **The picture of each gauge says its band in words to a screen reader, the two ends it is counted between, and whether it was asked for**: nothing of it is written beside the steps, so this is where whoever hears the page is told where the area sits. A gauge with no peg says that it has five empty steps and no peg. The two words of what is not whole and of what is not known are read, and their marks are kept from a screen reader. No line opens, and no key of a source stands on one: the fact of each is under the working | `RankedArea.strip`, `.contributions`, `.legs` and `.budget`. The spec that was ranked, for what was asked for and in what order, and `places` for the name of each place. `Tag.label`, `.low_end` and `.high_end`, and `Metric.short_label`, `.polarity`, `.family` and `.dimension`, from route 11. The `tag` fact at `StripMark.fact_id`, from route 3, for whether a band is whole. The `feature` fact of a measure, from route 3 or route 6, for its `band`, its `value` and its `standing`. The `budget_fit` fact, from route 3, for the amount as the API formatted it | A vibe the release does not name is left out, and so is a journey to a place with no name. Nothing to draw: no lines |
| No reason | A result says why it fits in no sentence, since 2026-09-27: the founder asked for "Why it fits" to go. Until then the first reason stood there, with the key of its source, and the picture of its vibe beside it where it was about one. Every reason is under the working | | |
| One trade-off | One sentence, after the word "Trade-off" and the drawing of a pair of scales. No key of a source follows it: its source is under the working. Until 2026-09-27 a sentence about a journey had "Over your limit of 30 minutes" or "Within your limit of 35 minutes" under it. The sentence says by how much a journey is over, so it is said once, and which way each journey falls is in the table of journeys, under the working. Where its figure is not whole, as a band that rests on part of what its vibe needs or a journey that was estimated, the sentence says so itself, in the API's words, and the two words are not said of it a second time. A vibe that is the trade-off has its line among the lines, as any vibe that was asked for has | `Explanation.trade_off.text` | `null`, or a sentence about something the ranking does not say the area does badly: "Burro found no trade-off." Where what the service wrote of the result could not be loaded, the result says so under the word. Where the service wrote nothing of the area, and nothing is waited for, no heading is drawn over nothing |
| The ways on | In the heading, "Add to compare", which puts the area in the bar of areas to compare, and becomes "Added to compare", which takes it out again. In the order of the page it comes after the rank, the name and the fit, which are read first and heard first, and before the town, the lines and the trade-off. "Show the working", which opens the result in place. "More like this", a link to the part of the area's page that lists the areas most like it | `AreaSummary.slug` | |

**The working, one press away.** Opening one result closes no other.

| Part | Shows | Filled from | When there is none |
|---|---|---|---|
| Where it is | The orientation sentence, the nearest station, and a `LocatorMap` | `Explanation.orientation.text`. The fact of kind `station` whose `template` is `station`, from route 6. Route 5 | No station row: the line is left out |
| Why it fits | Every reason the API gives, three at the most, in the order it gave them, each with "Source". Until 2026-09-27 the first stood on the result, and the working held the second and the third | `Explanation.reasons[].text`, and the fact at `fact_ids[0]` | No reason: "Burro found nothing that this area does well enough to give as a reason." Where the service wrote nothing of the area the part is left out |
| The source of the trade-off | What the sentence of the trade-off is about, by the name the API gives the fact, and "Source". The sentence stands on the result and is not said a second time, and the figures of its fact are not laid out again | The fact at `Explanation.trade_off.fact_ids[0]` | No trade-off: the part is left out |
| The figures behind each vibe | The fact of every vibe the API gave with the result, of those that were asked for and of the others, laid out as a fact is, each with "Source". A band that rests on part of what its vibe needs says what it was worked out from, in the API's words, after the mark of what is not whole. The vibe of the trade-off has its source under the trade-off, and is not laid out here | The `tag` fact at each `StripMark.fact_id`. Without it, `StripMark.band`, `.spread_low` and `.spread_high`, and the ends of the `Tag` | No fact in hand, as on a row, or on a card whose facts failed to come: the band the ranking gave and the ends it is counted between, under the names the columns of its fact bear. Under them one link, drawn with the key, leads to the page of the area, which holds the source and the date of each: "See the source and the date of each figure on the page for" the area. While the facts of a card are waited for, a line is held for each. No vibe the release names: the part is left out |
| What there is no figure for | First what it means for the fit, where the fit is not whole, after the mark and the two words that stand beside the fit on the result: how many of the things that count the fit is based on, how many of those nobody chose, what was asked for and has no figure, what nobody chose and has none, each named in quotation marks, that the journeys count for nothing where none has a time, that the budget counts for nothing where it is a guide and the area has no cost figure, and each firm limit that could not be held against the area. A journey and a budget are said once, each in the sentence that is its own: until 2026-09-27 "You asked for Budget" stood over the sentence that said a firm budget could not be checked. Then one sentence for each thing missing, each with "Source" | `contributions`, `untested_filters` and `legs`. `Explanation.missing[].text` | Nothing missing, and the fit whole: the part is left out. While the sentences are waited for, a line is held for each: the ranking says how many |
| Journeys | One row a place: its name, how, the typical time and the time if a service is just missed, and "within" or "over" the longest the person set. With two places or more, a line under them says which journey the fit uses, or that none of them counts. A link leads to how a journey is timed, on Methods | `RankedArea.legs[]`. `Commute.max_minutes` from the spec. The name from `placeNames` (section 5.3). The journey the fit uses is the leg named by `fact_ids[0]` of the `commute` contribution, or the average where `commute_combine` is `mean` | `beyond_cutoff`: "More than N minutes", N from `limits.cutoff_minutes`. `missing`: "No journey time in this data". `estimated`: the band in words, "Likely within your limit", "Borderline for your limit" or "Likely beyond your limit", then the line "This is an estimate that Burro worked out from the distance, and not a time from a timetable.", and no minutes. It is never said to be within its limit or over it (contract 6.10) |
| Cost | The range, the middle, what it is for, the month, and the confidence as a word with one to three pips. The word "Confidence" leads to what each word means, on Methods. The person's budget as a mark on the range. Every bar of a list is drawn on one scale. Where the cost is one number: the number, what it is of, the year of sales, the budget as a mark beside the number where one is set, whether the budget is below, above or at it, and the line that says what is not known of it | The `cost` fact for the spec's `tenure` and `budget.segment`, from route 6: `slots.lower`, `.upper`, `.median`, `.segment`, `.as_of`, `.confidence`. The bar's geometry from the `CostEstimate` of every card in hand. `Budget.amount` | "No cost figure in this data". **A visit draws nothing of it**: a visitor chose no kind of home, and until 2026-09-27 read under the working of every result that a figure was missing |
| Score breakdown | A table, closed at first: each thing that counts, its weight, its share, what it adds, and what the data says of it, with "Source". Under it, a line that says every figure is rounded down. The rows are in the order the API gave them | `Contribution[]`, without `utility`. Labels from `Metric.label` and `Tag.label`, and "Journey" and "Budget" for the other two. What the data says is the slots of the fact at `fact_ids[0]`, where it is in hand | The thing has no figure: "No figure". It has one and the fact is not in hand, as on a row: "Has a figure" |
| Sources and dates | Under the working every sentence and every figure ends in a "Source" button, and on the result as it is first shown none does: so the key of a source is one press from a result, and what it opens to a second. It opens, in place: each source's name once, linked to `/sources#source_id`, with who published it, then "Data from" with the date, once, and "Made-up data" when it is. The date is the date of what is said, and never of one source: it stands after the sources, and not beside each. A period is written as a date is at both its ends. Where a publisher asks that its own statement of credit stands wherever a figure made from its data is shown, the statement stands under the name of its source, as the API brings it with the fact (`FactSource.attribution`): Transport for London asks it. For a vibe it also says, in the API's words, whose recipe it is and that the weights are a judgement: the short sentence of a result leaves both out, and they are one press away. The date of a vibe is the span of its parts. The foot of the list names the release and the engine that made the ranking | `Fact.sources[]`, `Fact.as_of`, `Fact.synthetic`. `Fact.slots.made_from` and `.judgement` of a vibe. `Meta.release_id` and `Meta.engine_version` of the answer that brought the ranking | A figure with no fact in hand is not shown. A journey is the one exception (section 13, gap 2) |
| Actions | Open the area page, show on the map, hide this area. **What shows an area on the map says what it did**, in a line under the three that is heard as it is written, and the focus goes to the box the map opened for the area, which is brought into sight with it and leads back to the list. Until 2026-09-27 the press said nothing to whoever cannot see the map, and on a phone left the focus 1,492 px under the window. Where the page draws no map the line says so, and the focus stays on what was pressed | `AreaSummary.slug`. `AreaEdit` with `exclude` | |

**Under the list stand two things that say what the list does not hold.**

| Under the list | It says | From |
|---|---|---|
| How many are listed | "20 of the 21 areas ranked are listed here. The table of all areas holds every one." It is drawn once every listed result is on the page. Until then the button "Show 10 more" says that there is more | `areas_ranked` and `areas_listed` of routes 2 and 10. Nothing is said where they are equal |
| The areas that are not ranked | A button, closed at first, that says how many there are: "3 areas are not ranked". It stands on the line of "Show 10 more" where there is room, and takes a line of its own when it is opened. Opened: one line that says they have no fit and no rank, and then each area by its name, which leads to its page, with why it is not ranked in words and, under "No figure in this data for", each thing it lacks by the name the API gives it, what the person asked for before the settings nobody chose. The areas this search cannot place come first, and then the ones the data ranks for no search | `unranked` of routes 2 and 10: `reason` and `missing`. `Metric.label` and `Tag.label` of route 11. An area, a reason or a thing the page has no name for is left out, and never shown as its code |

An area that Burro cannot place on what counts in a search is in no result. It once came first, with a fit of 88, on its journey and its rent alone. The engine now ranks an area only where it has a figure for half of the character that counts (contract 6.6), and the page lists the rest apart, so that a person sees they are there and why. The map draws them with dots, its card says why in words, and the table of every area says it in the row of each.

The working of a row holds the figures behind its gauges, what its fit is based on, where the fit is not whole, the journeys, how the fit is worked out, and the actions. It has no reasons and no profile, because route 3 explains five results and route 6 is asked for the first five, and opening it asks nothing of the service. So a row has no trade-off, and the source of each of its gauges is on the page of its area: its working gives the band of each as the ranking gave it and leads there, since the key of a source stands on no result. Until 2026-09-27 it said nothing of a gauge, and a band of a row led to no source. Where another search left the fact of a gauge in hand, its key opens in place, as on a card. A row never says that a band is not whole: said of a row only where its fact happened to be in hand, it came and went as other searches were made.

- **Fit is rounded down.** `score` is shown as a whole number, rounded down, so 78.78 is "78 of 100". A fit is then never said to be more than it is, and the figure always falls in the band the map's legend gives for it. Every figure of the score breakdown is rounded down the same way, and a line under the table says so, because what the things add can then come to a little less than the fit.
- **One measure says how complete a result is.** It counts the things that have a figure. There is no bar: a bar once drew `weight_coverage`, which is another measure, so that "5 of the 10" stood beside a bar 77% wide. `weight_coverage` has no fact behind it and is not shown. Until 2026-09-27 the count was a sentence directly after the fit, and a firm limit that could not be tested was said there too, with the mark and the weight of the trade-off. Since then two words say beside the fit that it is not whole, and the sentences are under the working: **a firm limit that could not be held against an area is said on the result in the same two words as any fit that is not whole**, which [ADR 0046](../adr/0046-a-result-shows-what-was-asked-for-as-a-name-and-a-gauge-and-the-source-of-every-figure-stands-under-its-working.md) counts among what was given up.
- **How an area scores on a thing is never shown.** A contribution's `utility` is, for a feature, the percentile it is ranked by over 100, which the contract says is never printed: where areas tie it is untrue of the release (contract 7.2). The breakdown once printed it as "67 of 100", a few lines under a sourced sentence that said "quieter than 65% of the 20 areas". It now shows the fact's own slots, which are literally true and have a source. `figuresNotFrom` holds an opened breakdown to that.
- **Nothing the area does well is put under the word "Trade-off".** The API picks the trade-off, and gives none where an area does nothing badly (contract 7.5). The website checks it all the same against the ranking it holds, because the heading is the website's own word: a sentence is shown there only if the thing it is about counts in the search and either is worth less to the area than `limits.trade_off_max_utility`, which route 11 serves and is 0.35, or falls short, as a home over the budget or a journey over its limit does. Anything else is left out, and the card says that Burro found no trade-off. The website holds no copy of the figure. A test shows every trade-off the API recorded, so that the check is never stricter than the engine, and another holds every recorded reason to be worth a half or more.
- **A table inside a card is stacked on a narrow screen.** Under 40rem a journey becomes a block: the place, then whether it is within the limit, then how and how long, each under its own name. So does each row of the breakdown. Neither scrolls sideways, which once left the verdict off the screen. Each is one table either way, and every part says its role again, as in the comparison. The table of every area is stacked too: it was 512 px wide in a box of 358, so that a fit read "83 of 10". Each area is a block: its rank, its name and the way to show it on one line, and under them its borough, its fit and its status, each under its own name. It is stacked by the width of its own box and not of the screen (`@container`), because beside the list on a desk its box is as narrow as a phone: there it was 527 px wide in a box of 440, with the way to show an area out of sight.
- **What counts is not called what was asked for.** The settings nobody chose count too, for less, and are among the contributions. So the card says "things that count in your search", as the comparison says "What counts", and never "things you asked for".
- **An edit that was not applied says what it was about.** A feature, a vibe, a place and an area are named by the API. A budget, a tenure and the journeys are named in site copy (`REJECTED_PART`), so that "That changed nothing." never stands alone. **What a sentence asked for and was refused for its place or its number is named among what was left out.** "That number is outside what Burro accepts." was said of "within 2 minutes" of a place, in a line of its own under the chips, with no name of what it was about: the answers name no place that is not in the search. Which number, and where to go on, is more than that line has room for: said there in full, the first result of a search with a budget and two vibes ended 47 px under the first screen of a phone. So the line of what was left out names it, as "A journey", "A journey to" its place or "What you can pay", and why is one press away (`LEFT_OUT.why.range`, `LEFT_OUT.why.place`). What a control asked for and was refused is said beside the control, which shows what it was about, and in the list as it was.
- **Every "Source" button of a working says what it is the source of.** The sentence for a thing with no figure is named by that thing, the fact of a vibe by the vibe, and the source of the trade-off by the area, so that no two buttons of a working have one name.
- **The way to compare is on every card and every row, and the bar of areas stays within reach of it.** The button says what it would do, "Add to compare", and what it has done, "Added to compare", so that whether an area is chosen is never told by colour alone. Its name begins with what is seen on it and ends in the area, so that it can be spoken to. The bar takes no room until an area is chosen, so that the answer comes first. It then stays at the foot of the screen: in a browser the tray was once 5,570 px up the page from the fourth card's button. It is a box of the look, and has four places, which is the most a comparison takes. Each area that is chosen has one, with its town, its name and the cross that takes it out again, in the order they were chosen. Each place that is left is kept empty, so that the bar is as wide with one area as with four. What a town is, and is not, was said once in the bar until the evening of 2026-09-26, and is said there no longer. It holds one button. With one area chosen it says what to do next, which is to choose one more. With two to four the button says how many it compares. When four are chosen the buttons that would add a fifth are switched off, and each says why where it stands, as the bar does. The page scrolls what takes the focus clear of the bar, by its `scroll-padding-bottom`. The bar comes, and grows, at the foot of the screen, which may be under the pointer that pressed the button: a press the browser counts as the second of a double press lands on nothing of it. Nothing of the bar, and nothing of a result, says which area is the better: nobody wins a comparison. **How high the bar is, with a town for each area**, measured on 2026-09-26: on a phone 111 px with one area, 116 with two and 160 with three or four, which stand in two rows, and on a desktop 123.5 with one and 129.5 with two to four, with a `scroll-padding-bottom` of 176 px at both sizes. Before it held a town it was 94 px with two areas whose names take one line, 118 where they take two, 161 with four, and 96 on a desktop.
- **A fit never stands alone where it rests on part of what counts.** The pin, the card of the map and the table of every area say "This fit is based on 4 of the 9 things that count in your search." beside the fit, for every area the ranking ranks, from the `counted` and `present` the API gives with each `Score` (section 13, gap 18, met). `basedOn` in `src/lib/search/card.ts` is the one place that says it. On a result two words say it beside the fit, "approx data", and the sentence is under the working. Where no journey of an area has a time, the journeys count for nothing in its fit: the result has a line for the journey that says "no data", the two words stand beside the fit, and under the working the sentence says so, and again under the journeys in place of the line that says which one counts. A journey that has a time counts, and is the trade-off where it is over its limit, whatever the other journeys lack (section 13, gap 22, met).
- **A place is shown by its name, everywhere.** Every answer of routes 1, 2, 9 and 10 names every place of the spec it returns (`places`), and the chips, the journeys, the settings and what a share holds are drawn from those names. No place is shown by a number (section 13, gap 1, met).
- **What a sentence is of runs into it.** On a result "Trade-off" stands at the head of its sentence, which runs beside it and then under it. A heading above the sentence cost a line of every card. Until 2026-09-27 "Why it fits" stood so too, and the source followed the last word of each.
- **A link to an area's page is not fetched ahead of time.** The card's link and the table's are followed when pressed. Fetching them before would tell the website's own server which areas a search led to.

A sentence whose `origin` is `model` carries the words "Written by AI, checked against the source". None is today. A fact from route 6 has no sentence, so `FactRow` lays its slots out in fixed columns, chosen by `template`. A money slot gets its `£`, a range its "to", and nothing else is added. A column that only repeats the name of its row is left out, as the kind of home is on an area's page, where it names the row. A template the website was not built with shows no column, and never stops the page.

| `template` | Columns, left to right |
|---|---|
| `feature`, `feature_crime` | `label`, `slots.value`, `slots.standing`. Under a `feature_crime` row, the caveat of contract 7.3, word for word |
| `vibe` | `label`, `band`, `low_end` to `high_end`, `compared`, `span`. Where the band rests on part of the recipe, `known` and `parts`. Under it, `judgement`: that the recipe is Burro's own and the weights are a judgement |
| `vibe_range` | `label`, `spread_low` to `spread_high`, `low_end` to `high_end`, `span`. Where it rests on part of the recipe, `known` and `parts`. Under it, `judgement` |
| `vibe_unknown` | `label`, `known`, `parts`. Under it, in site copy, that Burro cannot place the area. No band is shown |
| `cost_rent`, `cost_buy` | `slots.segment`, `lower` to `upper`, `median`, `as_of`, `confidence` |
| `budget_under`, `budget_over`, `budget_at` | `upper`, `amount`, `margin` with "under" or "over". At the budget there is no `margin`: under "Against your budget" stands `verdict`, the API's own word. The same holds of a middle price and of a middle rent |
| `travel_pt`, `travel_other`, `travel_pt_over`, `travel_other_over`, `travel_beyond` | `place`, `mode`, then `typical` and `missed`, or `minutes`, or "more than" `cutoff`. Where the search sets a limit, `limit`, and `margin` under or over it. A journey that takes the minutes of its limit has no `margin`: under "Against your limit" stands `verdict`. A difference of nothing is never drawn as a figure |
| `station`, `station_nearby` | `name`, `walk`, `lines` |
| `area`, `missing` | `name` and `borough`. `label` |
| `missing_journey` | `name`, `place` |
| `travel_estimated` | `place`, `mode`, `limit`, `verdict`, `estimated`. No slot holds minutes of its own: an estimate is a band against the limit |
| `likeness`, `likeness_same` | `other`, `same`, `measures`, `family`. On the page of an area it is not laid out in columns: it is the contract's sentence, filled from these slots |

### 4.1 What a results page holds before anything is opened

A results page is at most three screens of a desktop and five of a phone before anything is opened, and the first result begins on the first screen (slice 1, decision 6). The two screens measured are 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844, so the limits are 2,700 and 4,220 px of `document.documentElement.scrollHeight`. **Since a result holds less, every page that was measured on a phone is within its limit but one, and every page of five results or more that was measured on a desktop is still over it**, by 464 to 1,967 px where it was by 1,916 to 3,368. Until 2026-09-27 nothing had been taken off a result to meet the limit: the founder then asked for less on a result, for its own sake ([ADR 0046](../adr/0046-a-result-shows-what-was-asked-for-as-a-name-and-a-gauge-and-the-source-of-every-figure-stands-under-its-working.md)).

**Measured on 2026-09-27, of the website as it stands since the founder walked it a third time**, in Chrome driven by software, at 1440 by 900 and at 390 by 844, on the made-up city at engine 1.19.0, in CSS pixels from the top of the window. Each search was typed on the first screen, or chosen there among the examples, and sent, and nothing was opened or scrolled after it. A shared link was made of a search and opened. A first result was shown 3.83 to 3.90 seconds after the press, which is the wait, and an eighth of a second after it where less movement was asked for. What each was before is of the same search, measured the same way on 2026-09-26: the table after this one has it.

| Search | Desktop: page, first result | Phone: page, first result |
|---|---|---|
| Before a search | 1,033, where it was 1,009. The field from 647.5 to 720.5, and the two helpers from 770.5 to 814.5 | 1,304. The field from 708 to 779. The two helpers from 1,011 to 1,055, under the first screen |
| Renting a 1 bed, a firm budget, leafy and quiet, a journey | 3,348, where it was 4,640. From 687 to 1,119, where it ended at 1,348.5 | 3,237, where it was 3,906. From 452 to 759, whole, with 85 to spare where 6 were |
| The same, with "for about" before the budget, which makes it a guide: ten results are listed | 4,629. From 649 to 1,081 | 4,265, which is 45 over the limit. From 452 to 759, whole, with 85 to spare |
| The first example, of renting | 4,667, where it was 6,068. From 669 to 1,101, the page 18 down | 4,200, where it was 4,578. From 408 to 715, whole, with 129 to spare |
| The second example, of buying | 4,555, where it was 4,928. From 613 to 1,045, the page 18 down | 4,041, where it was 4,676. From 408 to 715, whole, with 129 to spare |
| The third example, of bars and a station | 4,233, where it was 6,005. From 631 to 1,009, the page 18 down | 3,808, where it was 4,361. From 376 to 640, whole, with 204 to spare |
| "Leafy, renting, 30 minutes to Pellam", of which Burro takes the first of three places | 4,159, where it was 5,549. From 687 to 1,025 | 3,669, where it was 4,100. From 408 to 625, whole, with 219 to spare |
| "leafy and quiet", whose first result has no figure for something that counts | 4,547, where it was 5,860. From 575 to 1,014.5 | 4,052, where it was 4,395. From 364 to 688.5, whole, with 155.5 to spare, where it ended 26.5 under the first screen |
| A sentence of seven things: five vibes, a budget and a journey | 3,802, where it was 5,324. From 649 to 1,165 | 3,556, where it was 4,578. From 376 to 757.5, whole, with 86.5 to spare, where it ended 84 under |
| The founder's own sentence, of a dozen things | 3,427, where it was 5,370. From 687 to 1,143 | 3,330, where it was 4,629. From 404 to 753, whole, with 91 to spare, where it ended 64.5 under |
| A sentence that is no plain list, which holds a firm budget to buy | 3,164, where it was 4,708. From 725 to 1,079 | 3,035, where it was 3,946. From 480 to 728, whole, with 116 to spare, where it ended 14 under |
| A visit, of a sentence: somewhere lively, near a station | 4,175, where it was 5,651. From 631 to 1,009 | 3,812, where it was 4,097. From 364 to 649, whole, with 195 to spare |
| A search with two journeys and a firm budget, which ranks one area | 1,564. From 651.4 to 1,161.9 | 1,827. From 376 to 759.5, whole, with 84.5 to spare |
| The page a shared link opens, of the plain search | 3,324, where it was 4,616. From 663 to 1,095 | 3,289, where it was 3,958. From 452 to 759, whole, with 85 to spare |
| The page a shared link opens, of the founder's sentence | 3,365, where it was 5,233. From 625 to 1,081 | 3,354, where it was 4,637. From 376 to 725, whole, with 119 to spare, where it ended 20.5 under |

- **On a phone the first result is whole on the first screen after every search that was measured**, which are the fourteen of the table. The least it has to spare is 84.5 px, so one line more over the answer would leave it whole after each of them. On 2026-09-26 it was whole after seven of twelve.
- **It is not held to be whole after every search.** What was measured is a first result of four gauges at the most, which is what a result draws of the vibes that were asked for, and a card of four gauges is 381.5 to 458 px high on a phone: a line with a picture at each end is as high as its pictures, 38 px where it was about 26, and the pictures cannot be drawn smaller and stay sharp. The first result of such a search begins at 376, so it is whole whichever of those cards comes first, with 10 px to spare at the least. A result that holds a line for a thing with no figure under its four gauges was not measured.
- **On a desk the first result begins on the first screen after every search, and ends on it after none.** It begins at 575 to 725 of 900, and ends 109 to 265 px under a window 900 high, where it ended 225 to 607 under.
- **A card is 338 to 570.5 px high on a desk and 217 to 458 on a phone**, where it was 491.5 to 786 and 246 to 528. A row is 232 to 277.5 and 155 to 208.5. The first result of the plain search holds 52 words, where it held 124 on a desk and 80 on a phone.
- **Every result holds four things to press**, where a first result held eight to ten: its name, the way to compare, its working, and what is like it. No line of a result opens, and no key of a source stands on one.
- **Every results page of five results or more is over the limit of a desk**, by 464 to 1,967 px. On a phone such a page is 3,035 to 4,265, where the limit is 4,220: one is over it, by 45 px, which is that of the plain search with its budget as a guide.
- **The steps of every line of every result began at one place down each page**, of the ten searches whose every gauge was measured: at 338.2 px on a desk and at 211.2 on a phone after seven of them, and further in after the three whose column of names is wider. That is at 345.5 and 218.5 after the search that ranks one area, at 353 and 226 after the sentence of seven things, and at 421 and 238 after a second sentence of seven things, which names two journeys. On 2026-09-26 they began at 362.5 to 370 on a desk and at 141.7 on a phone, where the names of the two ends stood under the steps.
- **What stands over the first result of the plain search, on a phone:** the banner, in one line; the name board, from 38 to 90; and the search box, from 94 to 444, which holds the label and the field, the line of what happened, and under the plate of what Burro understood five chips in four rows, from 226 to 428. Then 8 px of grass. On a desktop the box stands from 165 to 590.5, with five chips in three rows, and "Refine search" from 602.5 to 646.5, with what says that areas can be compared on its line. They stand where they stood on 2026-09-26.
- **The line of what was left out of a search is 24 px high on a phone and 30 on a desktop, closed.** It stands in the box, under the line of what happened and over what Burro understood: after the founder's own sentence from 226 to 250 on a phone, with what Burro understood from 254 under it.
- **The chips of a search of many things fold at both sizes**: after the founder's own sentence three chips are in sight on a desktop and two on a phone, in two rows, and the button that opens the rest stands on the line of the plate, over them.
- **Opened, "Refine search" is within one screen of a desk and not of a phone**, with every group closed: from its bar to the foot of what it holds it is 712.5 px at 1440 by 900 and 898 at 390 by 844, as it was on 2026-09-26.
- **The box Burro hops in is higher than the answer that takes its place.** On a first search it keeps the room that what Burro understood and a first result took before a result came to hold less: 580 px on a phone, from 246 to 826, and 462 on a desktop, from 376.5 to 838.5. After the plain search the first result of a phone ends at 759, which is 67 px over where his box ended, and after "leafy and quiet" at 688.5. Under his box on a phone stands the map, from 834 while he hops and from 969 once the answer is in, since "Refine search" comes between the two: it is under the first screen both times, and nothing in sight moves but what takes his place. On a desktop the first result begins at 687, which is 310.5 px under where his box began, with what Burro understood and "Refine search" over it. The two heights are two lines of `SearchApp/Wait.module.css`.
- **What was built a second way was measured too**, earlier that day, and [the design of the look](look.md), section 4, has each: the trade-off in a box, the two ends of a gauge named, the vibes nobody asked for beside those that were, and what a fit is based on said in full on the result. What stood over the list was then as it stood on 2026-09-26, and a sentence that is no plain list was read as the service read it before it came to say what waits for a person. So what each row there says one way costs against the other stands, and where a first result begins and ends after such a sentence is as this table has it.
- No page was wider than its window, and after no press was the focus on the page as a whole: it was on the button that was pressed.

jsdom lays nothing out, so no test measures a height. A test holds what the heights depend on, and the heights are measured by hand (section 9, check 14). `test/search/budget.test.tsx` counts what is drawn before anything is opened, for six recorded searches: ten results at most, five of them cards; on a card a line for each thing that was asked for, the trade-off and three ways on, and four things to press; on a row no sentence; six chips at most and the usual settings, each in short; nothing open; 61 controls at most, where it held 90 until 2026-09-27 and a card held one reason and the key of each source; thirteen at most between the box and the first result; and twelve controls at most in the settings when they are opened and no group is. Before a search it counts the box, three helpers and nothing that a helper opens: ten controls at most, where it counted thirty. `scripts/check-pages.mjs` counts the search page as it was built, and was brought to the two ways in: sixteen controls at most before a search, of which 14 were drawn, which were two links that skip, the two tabs, the box and Search, the way to how words are handled, two helpers and five of the map. Since the evening of 2026-09-26 it may draw fourteen, and draws 13: the way to how words are handled went, and the check fails where the box of the search page says anything of who reads what is typed, or holds a link. It counts too one list of two tabs, of which the first is chosen and holds the box; the panel of the second in the page, holding nothing until its tab is pressed; one line of two helpers, each closed; and for a browser with scripts off both ways in, each under its name, with eleven controls at most that the helpers keep and eight words of the shelf. A change that draws more at first fails one of them, and is to be measured again before a count is raised. From 80rem the settings stood open beside the answer, with every group closed: they were counted with the page, and not as something that was opened. **What is said of `test/search/budget.test.tsx` is what it held until 2026-09-26**: the usual settings among the chips, three helpers, and the settings counted where they stood open. It holds since: before a search ten controls at most in the first way in, of which none leads to the methods, and in the second the settings with two groups open, which draw 24 controls at most; after a search six chips at most, of which none is of the usual settings, thirteen controls at most between the box and the first result, fourteen where something was left out, 90 in the page, and fourteen in the settings as they open with every group closed. A count is raised only once the page was measured in a browser.

### 4.2 What the page of an area holds before anything is opened

The page of an area is at most four screens of a desktop and seven of a phone before anything is opened: 3,600 and 5,908 px of `document.documentElement.scrollHeight` at 1440 by 900 and 390 by 844. It opens with the portrait, and ends with "Go and look". **As the website stands most pages are over that limit**: 21 of the 24 of the made-up city on a desktop, and twenty on a phone. Nothing was taken out of a page to meet it, and which gives way is the founder's to say.

| On the page, in order | Drawn at first | One press away |
|---|---|---|
| The head, in one box: the name, what stands beside it and their source. Then the town of the area, with the line that says it is drawn from four of the area's vibes and is not a picture of the place ([ADR 0034](../adr/0034-the-town-of-an-area-is-drawn-by-rule-and-is-no-picture-of-the-place.md)): on a narrow screen it is said in short, in one sentence that says both. The line went from a result, from the bar of areas and from the head of a comparison on the evening of 2026-09-26, and stays here and in the key to the drawings. By the town, the way to compare the area, as plain as on a result, with a line that says what comparing does | All of it. The parts of the town are not written out under it: the portrait under the head says every band, and the drawing says them to a screen reader | |
| The portrait, under "Character" | What the area is like in short, in five lines at most, with what opens their sources. Beside it, where it is: the picture, the areas next to it, the nearest station, and the journeys of the search that is open. Then one line for each vibe the area is placed on, under the heading of the list the API puts it in, and one line that names the vibes it cannot be placed on. Under "More of these than most areas", the way to a search for those vibes | What each vibe is made of, by pressing its line. What is known of each vibe that cannot place the area, by one press for all of them |
| "On this page" | The list of what follows | |
| "More like this", "What homes cost", "What is measured here", "Sources on this page" | The heading of each, which opens it | All that each holds |
| The census figures, under the heading the API gives them, where a census is served | The heading, which opens it | The words that offer it, and then the figures, which are asked for when it is opened (section 2.1) |
| "Go and look" | The stations, where the data names any, and what every vibe cannot see | What each vibe that is shown cannot see, and the way to the page of vibes |
| The tray of areas to compare | Nothing, until an area is chosen. It is then at the foot of the screen | |

As on the search page, no test measures a height. `test/area/budget.test.tsx` holds what the heights depend on, for every area of the release: that nothing is open, that the five long parts are closed and the portrait is not, that the page opens with the portrait and ends with "Go and look", that each vibe the area is placed on is one line, that what it cannot be placed on is said once, that what it is like in short is five lines at most, and that no more is drawn than was measured with a little room, which is 190 lines and 40 controls at most. The room is used up: a release carries fourteen vibes since Well connected and the two that count who lived in an area joined, and the chains of grocers, gyms and coffee beside them, so what "Go and look" says of an area grew, the census of an area and its household income are each a part that is closed at first, which is three lines more for each, and the page of the centre draws 190 lines. It was measured at 151 lines with eleven vibes, and has not been measured in a browser since. `scripts/check-pages.mjs` holds the same of each page as it was built.

**How the page is drawn.** It stands on the meadow, and every sentence, figure, link and fold of it stands in a box or within a plain edge: a test walks every piece of text of all 24 recorded pages, and fails on one that stands on the grass. The page itself draws Burro nowhere. What every page stands in draws him, since the website was mended on 2026-09-26: in the name board on a screen 40rem wide or wider, and over the foot of the page on a narrower one.

| Part | Drawn as |
|---|---|
| The head | A box of the look. The name in the face of names. The town at the far end from the name, 112 by 64 on every screen, so that it is no higher than the name. An area that is not ranked says so as a notice |
| The portrait | What opens it, which is what the area is like in short, where it is and the sentence over the lists, in one box. Then each list of vibes in a box of its own, under its name on a plate of ink |
| The line of a vibe | The small drawing of the vibe before its name. Five steps with a peg, between the one picture of each end of the vibe, of every vibe since 2026-09-27: until then a vibe that runs one way had none, and an end of a scale was two pictures side by side. A chequered step, and the mark of what is not whole with "approx data", where the band rests on part of its recipe. It is laid out by the width of its list, in the size of the text, and not by the width of the screen. What says that an area varies between two bands is broken as a sentence is: set on one line it was 384 px wide, and the pages of three areas were 26 px wider than a phone |
| What is closed until it is pressed | The browser's own `details`, drawn as a strip from edge to edge of one box: sand while closed and amber while open, with the arrow of the look, which turns in one step |
| A source that is written out | With the key of a source before it, which is dress and opens nothing. "Made-up data" after it is a small notice |
| The census and the household income | Their fold, their failure and their one button are drawn by the style sheet of the page. Their tables are as they were, in the colours of the text of the page: the ten rules of section 2.1 stand, and their tests pass unchanged |
| "Go and look" | A box, with its heading on a board of hedge green |
| The ways on | Buttons of the look. "Search for areas" is the one in cobalt |
