**Where the website is known to fall short.** The statement of accessibility said each of these to a visitor until it went. No page says them now.

| Where | What falls short |
|---|---|
| The rabbit | He moves on every page for as long as it is open, and nothing on a page stops him. A person who is troubled by movement, and whose system does not ask for less of it, must find the setting of their system or of their browser, and no page says that there is one |
| Scripts off | A search, a comparison and a shared link need scripts. With scripts off the first page shows both ways to begin and cannot search, and its map goes on saying that it is loading. The census figures and the household income of an area are loaded when they are opened, which needs scripts too |
| A failure before anything is drawn | The page is the framework's own: it does not say what language it is in, and is not in Burro's words. It shows no figure and names no place |
| The first screen of a phone, before a search | The field is on it. The two helpers under the box are not: a low map stands over the two tabs |
| Where the focus is, after a press that changes the search | It goes to what Burro understood, which is named to a screen reader and has no ring drawn round it |
| The order of the keyboard among the helpers | What a helper opens is not the next stop after it, because the other helper comes first. What a word of the shelf opens is reached after the words that follow it. What a hand opened over its line comes before the line in the page, so whoever goes on by keyboard from what they pressed goes away from what it opened |
| A link to another page | The focus begins at the head of the new page, and is not given to its heading |
| The map, by keyboard | A pin that has left the window of the map can still take the focus, and nothing is then drawn to show where the focus is |
| A scale on a result | The end that was asked for is said to a screen reader, in the name of the picture. For the eye nothing on a result says which end was asked for, since the names of the two ends are not drawn there: the chip of the search says it |
| The two ends of a gauge, on a result | Each is a picture, and its name is not drawn. A person who does not take the meaning of a picture finds it in the key to the drawings, on another page, and under the working, where the fact of the vibe names both ends |
| A figure that is not whole, on a result | Two words say so, "approx data", and nothing in sight says how much of the figure there is, or of a fit why it is not whole. A firm limit that could not be held against an area is said in the same two words. Each is under the working |
| The source of a figure, on a result | No key of a source stands on a result as it is first shown. The key is under the working, one press away, and what it opens to a second |
| A result, on every screen | It shows the vibes that were asked for alone, unless none was. The rest are under its working and on the page of the area |
| The title of the page, once a search is open | It is kept for a screen reader and is not drawn, with the heading of the results, so that the first result is whole on the first screen of a phone |
| Words that were not read | The page says that they were not, and can select them. It cannot say why |
| Contrast of an edge | The amber band of a banner is 1.6 to 1 against cream, where 3 to 1 is asked of an edge. The banner also stands on a line of ink. The line of sand under a row of a table is fainter still |
| The names on the map | The map names the areas it has room for. On a phone it names none before a search and a few after one |
| "Start again" on the page a shared link opens | It opens the search again as the link holds it, and does not begin an empty one |
| A first result of four gauges, on a phone | It is whole on the first screen after every search that was measured on 2026-09-27, and is not held to be after every search: where the highest card of four gauges comes first it has 10 px to spare, and a result that holds a line more, for a thing that was asked for and has no figure, was not measured |
| What a press opens, where it has room neither under what was pressed nor over it | Its head is brought into sight, and its foot is not: on a phone the card of a word of the shelf stood from 515.5 to 1,181 of 844 |
| A word of the shelf, as its card opens over it | It may move under the hand by a little: at 1440 by 900 it stood 11.5 px lower once its card had opened over the words |
| The box of the map, as a results page opens | On a screen lower than about 830 px its foot is under the screen until the page is scrolled: by 100.5 px at 1280 by 720. In a window 900 high it is whole since 2026-09-27 |
| A gauge that has no picture at its ends | The legend of the small map in the card of a word of the shelf is five swatches, each with its band in words. The table of every area and the card of the map say a band in words and draw no gauge, so neither says "approx data": the bands that come with the list of areas do not say whether a band is whole |
| A journey to a name that several places bear, in words that make its limit firm | It is taken as a firm limit, to the first of the places, and leaves out every area that is known to be further from that place: the service asks about such a journey in its own edit, as it was worded, and the page changes nothing of the edit but its place. [ADR 0012](../adr/0012-a-closed-vocabulary-and-a-guard-on-the-model.md) has a journey taken as a guide until a person makes it firm, and `A_LIMIT_THE_WORDS_MAKE_FIRM` does not reach this one |
| A press that moves the page | Where the bar of areas came over the button that chose an area, the page goes by as much as brings the button clear of the bar, and no further. And a press that leads to the map takes the page there, once, since 2026-09-27: "Show" in a row of the table of all areas, and on a narrow screen "Show on the map" under the working of a result. What was pressed goes from under the hand, by as little as brings the map into sight with the card of the area under it, and not at all where both are in sight. Beside the answer, "Show" in a row has the list bring the result of the area into sight as well: measured that day at 1440 by 900, the page went 2,862 px to the eighth result |
| Text twice as large, on a phone | Once a search is open the page is 82 px wider than the window, by the box of the search and its buttons. It was seen with the size set by software |
| How to report a problem | There is nowhere to send a report, and no page to say so on. [The launch checklist](../legal/data-protection-checklist.md), task 11, asks for an address |

**What nobody has tested.** A screen reader. Text at 200% zoom. Reflow at 320 px. Forced colours, but in the browser's emulation. The real size of a control on a touch screen. The map by keyboard alone. A comparison, the table of every area, the journeys of a result and the working of a fit, each on a narrow screen where rows are laid out as blocks. The two tabs, the groups of the settings, a switch, a slider drawn as a row of steps and a scale with two ends, with a screen reader or on a touch screen. The page of an area and the pages that explain, with a screen reader. A link into a fold of the methods in any browser but Chrome. The town of an area with a screen reader: down a list of ten results it says what it is made of ten times. Whether the bar of areas ever covers what has the focus, at a keyboard in a person's hands. Copying the link of a shared search in a real browser. The census and the household income with a screen reader, at 200% zoom and in forced colours. The two faces at other sizes of text. The drawings at a zoom between two whole steps. Anything that moves, as it moves. Any browser but Chrome, and any phone.

## 10. Privacy in the browser

| What | May be held in | Never in |
|---|---|---|
| The sentence typed | The textarea, and the body of the `POST` to route 1 that reads it: what is added after it is sent without it. A part of it may be selected in the textarea, where it already is | The store, a URL, storage, the title, the console, an error report, and anywhere on the page outside the textarea |
| Where the words of a sentence stand: the `spans` of a suggestion, and `unread` | The store, as offsets, until the box changes. They mean nothing without the text | A URL, storage, the console |
| How far into the box the search has read, and where the words that were last read begin (`box`) | The store, as counts of the characters of the box, and whether words that were read were changed since. They mean nothing without the box. Where the box was changed and by how much, and how many characters were handed over to be read, are told to the store as counts and are not kept: what is typed after the words that were read changes nothing the store holds. With them the store is told how what came into the box came, where it had been in the box before: that the keys that undo put it back, or the keys that do again, or that it was pasted. A fixed name of three, and not what came | A URL, storage, the console. No word is kept with them, no hash of one, no copy, and no length of a word |
| How many characters that were read were taken out of the box, and what the search knew of the box at the times the keys that undo and that do again lead back to (`gone`, `back` and `again` of `box`) | The store: a count, and for each of those times how many characters the box held, how many have come into it since, and how far the search had read. No more than 64 of each are kept. It is how words that those keys put back are known for what was read | A URL, storage, the console. No word is kept with them, and nothing worked out from one |
| Where what was last copied, cut or dragged from the box began, how many characters it was, and whether any of it had been read, or has been sent to be read since | The box itself, in memory, while it is drawn: two counts, and yes or no. What was copied is the clipboard's to hold, and the website never reads the clipboard | The store, a URL, storage, the console, an attribute |
| Which vibe a person looks at on the map, before a search | The page, while its card is open | Any request: the bands of every vibe come with the page. A URL, storage, the console |
| Which things a search holds, as the drawings a page asks for | The address of a drawing, which a browser asks the website's own host for where a chip, an offer or a line of that thing is drawn: `/art/thing-leafy.png`. It names a vibe or a measure by the id the service gives it, or the kind of a thing: a journey, a budget, renting or buying. It names no place, no figure and no word that was typed. So the log of whatever serves the website says which vibes a search holds, which it did not before the look. It is a part of what the spec holds, in an address, where the row of the spec below says that a spec is in none. Whether that may stand is the founder's to decide: [look.md](look.md) has the one line that draws every thing by its kind alone, and what would take the rest away | The address of a page, storage, the console, and any request to another origin |
| Which chips the row of chips last drew, and which searches it has drawn | The row, in memory, as the keys of chips, which are the search's own. It is how a chip is known to be new, so that it alone drops into its place | The store, a URL, storage, the console. No word, and nothing that was typed |
| Which of the two tabs is chosen, and which helper and which groups of the settings are open | The page, in memory, while it is open. Each goes when the page is loaded again. Until the evening of 2026-09-26 whether the rabbit was stopped was kept so too: nothing is kept of him since, for nothing stops him | A URL, storage, the console, any request |
| What was pressed in the settings, and where it stood in the window | The settings, in memory, from a press that changes the search until the answer to it is drawn: the element and a figure. It is how what was pressed is held where it stood | The store, a URL, storage, the console |
| What the page took of what Burro noticed, and what it took nothing of (`took`, `things` and `left` of `read`) | The store: a count, what each thing is of by the id the service gives it, and for what was left why, with the service's own words for it. None holds a word that was typed | A URL, storage, the console |
| Until when a first answer is held back | The search, in memory, as a time of the page's own clock, while a first answer is waited for | The store, a URL, storage, the console |
| The word a vibe was read from, where it has two meanings (`Assumption.word`) | The store and the chip. It is the reader's own word for the vibe, from its answer, and never what was typed | A URL, storage, the console |
| Which vibes a search is to start from, when "Search for these vibes" is pressed on an area's page | The session, as the ids of vibes, until the search page takes them. Then the body of one `POST` to route 2 | A URL, storage, the console. Which area it was pressed on is in no request |
| Which vibe a person reads on the page of vibes | The fragment of `/vibes#tag_id`, which a browser sends to no server | A path or a query string, storage, the console |
| Place search text | The field, and the body of one `POST` to route 8 | The same |
| A `place_id`, a place name | The store. The body of a `POST`. The page, as text | A URL, storage, the console |
| The spec, `spec_hash` | The store. The body of a `POST` | A URL, storage, the console |
| A `fact_id` | The store | A URL, an element's `id`, the console: a journey's holds a `place_id` |
| A `share_id` | The store, for a share that was opened. The session, for a link that was made. The fragment of `/s#share_id`. The path of the one call to route 10. The field of `SharePanel` the link is copied from. The clipboard, when the person presses "Copy link" | A path or a query string of the website, a link on the page, storage, the console |
| An area's slug | A URL. The person reached it by pressing, not typing | |
| The census figures of an area, and whether a person opened them | The page, while it is open. The path of the one call to route 13 holds the slug of the page | Storage, the console, the session, the address of the page, and any request but that one. The answer is `no-store` |
| The household income of an area, and whether a person opened it | The page, while it is open. The path of the one call to route 14 holds the slug of the page | As the census figures |
| Release id, engine version, `X-Request-Id` | Anywhere on the page | The console |
| Where accounts are on: the address a person signs in with | The field it is typed in, and the body of the one `POST` that asks for a link. The page in memory, while it is open, so that the next page can say where the link went. The page, as text, once the service has said who is signed in | A URL, storage, the console, the title |
| The token of a link to sign in | The fragment of `/sign-in/confirm#t=`, until the page has read it, which is at once. Then the page in memory, and the body of the two requests that use it | The address bar, once it was read. A path or a query string, storage, the console, a link on the page |
| A session, and what binds a link to a browser | Two cookies that the service sets and the website passes on, `__Host-burro_session` and `__Host-burro_link`. No script can read either | Anything a script holds. The website never reads a cookie |
| The id of a search that is kept, and of a session | The page in memory, and the body of the request that takes one away | A URL, storage, the console |

Rules the code keeps:

1. The website never touches `localStorage`, `sessionStorage`, IndexedDB, cookies, the Cache API or a service worker. Where accounts are on, a browser holds two cookies, which the service sets, the website's handler passes on, and no script of the website reads, sets or can read ([the design of accounts](accounts.md), section 5).
2. No analytics, no error reporter, and no script, style, font or image from another origin. The two faces and every drawing are files of the website's own. A content security policy in `next.config.ts` allows the website's own origin and the API's, and no other. Next's own telemetry is switched off in every npm script.
3. The prompt form has `method="post"`, and the textarea and the search field have no `name`, `autocomplete="off"` and `spellcheck="false"`. If scripts fail, pressing Enter cannot put the text in a URL, and the browser does not keep it for next time. Nor does it check the spelling: a browser's fuller spell check sends the text of a checked field to its maker, which is a request to another party that no content security policy can stop.
4. The website calls no `console` method. The error boundary shows fixed text and reads nothing from the error.
5. Navigation goes only to paths built by `src/lib/paths.ts`, which takes a slug or nothing. `Referrer-Policy` is `no-referrer`, and a link to a source's site is `noreferrer`.
6. A link to an area, a source or a comparison is not fetched ahead of time, so the website's own server is not told what a person is reading before they ask for it.

Each test plants a canary, a string found nowhere else, in the prompt, in the place search and in a place name of a stand-in answer. It then runs a whole search. `test/privacy/search.test.tsx` reads, asks, ranks, refines, fails, goes offline, and sends a sentence that is not plain, of which the page takes what it may and leaves the rest. `test/search/added.test.tsx` adds to a sentence that was read, changes a word of it, and tries again what could not be sent. `test/privacy/share.test.tsx` searches, chooses areas, makes a link twice, copies it and opens the comparison. `test/share/page.test.tsx` opens a link, and what is not one.

| Test | Proves |
|---|---|
| `test_typed_text_never_reaches_a_url` | The canary is in no `href`, no router call, no `history` call and no request URL |
| `test_a_place_name_never_reaches_a_url` | The same, for a place's name and its id |
| `test_nothing_is_ever_written_to_browser_storage` | No call to any storage of rule 1, in any state of section 3 |
| `test_typed_text_never_reaches_the_console` | No `console` call on any path, a thrown render error and a failed request among them |
| `test_typed_text_travels_only_in_a_post_body` | The canary is in the body of routes 1 and 8, and in no other request |
| `test_the_prompt_form_cannot_be_sent_as_a_get` | Rule 3, on the rendered form |
| `test_whatever_is_taken_is_a_way_the_service_gave_with_the_edits_it_gave`, `test_what_counts_recorded_crime_is_left_unless_the_service_says_the_persons_own_words_name_it`, `test_what_the_service_says_waits_for_a_person_is_never_taken_whatever_it_says_of_its_ways`, `test_what_counts_who_lived_somewhere_is_never_taken_and_is_said_to_be_left` | What the page takes of what was noticed is a way the API gave, with the edits the API gave, so what is sent holds nothing that was typed. What waits for a person is left, and said to be. Until the evening of 2026-09-26 two tests held that the words beside an offer were cut from the box and written nowhere else, and that nothing was ranked until a person chose: both were of a page that asked |
| `test_a_word_opens_the_card_of_its_vibe_and_sends_nothing` | Looking at a vibe makes no request and writes nothing down |
| `test_opening_it_sends_nothing_and_writes_nothing_down` | Opening "More like this" on an area's page makes no request and writes nothing down |
| `test_more_like_this_on_a_result_leads_to_the_areas_page_and_holds_nothing_that_was_typed` | The address a result leads to holds the slug of the area and the name of a part of its page, and nothing of the search |
| `test_pressing_it_opens_the_search_with_one_edit_for_each_vibe_and_nothing_typed` | A search started from an area sends the ids of vibes to route 2, names no area, and puts nothing in the address |
| `test_a_share_id_is_only_ever_in_the_fragment` | The link made is `/s#id`, no call holds the id, no link on the page leads to it, and the address and the history are untouched |
| `test_a_share_id_is_only_ever_in_the_fragment_and_in_the_one_call_that_opens_it` | An opened share's id is in the path of route 10 and in nothing the page draws |
| `test_nothing_the_person_typed_is_in_the_link` | The link is the website's origin, `/s` and the id, and holds no canary and no place |
| `test_what_is_not_an_id_is_never_sent_anywhere_and_never_shown` | A fragment that is not an id makes no call and is not repeated on the page |
| `test_the_link_to_a_comparison_holds_the_slugs_of_the_areas_and_nothing_else` | The address of a comparison holds `a=slug` and nothing of the search |
| `test_the_error_boundary_says_fixed_words_and_reports_nothing` | An error built from the canary shows and logs nothing of it |
| `test_the_store_holds_no_sentence` | No field of the store holds the canary after a search |
| `test_it_is_kept_as_counts_in_the_store_and_no_word_of_the_box_is_kept_anywhere` | With a canary in a sentence and in what is added after it: what the store holds of the box is counts and whether a thing is so, in every state it was in, and the canary is in the body of route 1 as `text` and nowhere else |
| `test_the_box_says_where_it_was_changed_in_counts_and_never_what_was_typed` | What the box tells the store of a change holds whole numbers, and no letter of what was typed |
| `test_what_the_box_says_of_how_words_came_is_a_name_of_few_and_never_a_word_of_them` | With a canary taken out of the box and put back, and copied and pasted: what the box tells the store holds whole numbers and a fixed name of three, and no letter of the canary |
| `test_what_is_kept_to_know_it_is_counts_and_whether_a_thing_is_so` | With a canary taken out of the box, and a part of it put back by the keys that undo: every leaf of what the store keeps of both is a number, yes, no or nothing |
| `test_the_spec_is_only_ever_one_the_api_returned` | Rule 2 of section 0: every spec in the store is identical to one in an answer |
| `test_nothing_is_loaded_from_another_origin` | The source holds no URL outside a short list, and the policy names two origins |
| `test_the_website_has_one_handler_which_passes_accounts_on_and_no_action_or_middleware` | Two files under `src/app` are a route handler, at the two places the routes of accounts are asked at, and each does nothing but hand the request to `lib/account/pass.ts`. No file says `use server`. Until accounts were built the test was `test_the_website_has_no_handler_action_or_middleware` |
| `test_no_example_names_a_place` | The example sentences hold no proper noun |

## 11. Folder layout, scripts and who builds what

```
apps/web/
  AGENTS.md  CLAUDE.md          rules of this package; CLAUDE.md is one line
  package.json  tsconfig.json  next.config.ts  eslint.config.mjs  jest.config.ts
  .gitignore                    package-lock.json, node_modules, .next
  art/                          every drawing, written as text, one character a pixel, in ten files.
                                README.md says how one is written and named
  public/art/                   the 155 pictures, made from the text by `gen:art`. Committed, never edited
  public/fonts/                 the two faces, in four files, and the licence of each beside it
  src/app/                      layout, page, [city]/[area], compare, s, vibes, methods, sources,
                                robots.ts, sitemap.ts, icon.svg, not-found, error, global-error
  src/components/               one folder a component: Name.tsx, Name.module.css, Name.test.tsx
  src/components/kit/           the kit of the look, one folder a part: section 7. drawings.ts is the one
                                file of it that reads the list of drawings
  src/components/Town/          the town of an area, its key, and the drawing alone
  src/content/                  site copy: states, unmet, rejections, labels, examples, banner, what the
                                look added: helpers, card, added, town, about, kit, and what the two
                                ways in added: ways. words.md says how each is written
  scripts/gen-api.mjs           writes and checks what is generated from the contract
  scripts/gen-art.mjs           makes the pictures and the list of their names from the text, and checks
                                that the committed ones are what the text gives. Plain Node, and no package
  scripts/check-pages.mjs       reads every page the build wrote, and fails on what is wrong with one
  src/lib/api/                  schema.d.ts and required.ts (both generated), operations.ts, client.ts (browser),
                                server.ts (build), send.ts, failure.ts, config.ts, synthetic.ts, recorded.ts
  src/lib/search/               state.ts, store.ts, flow.ts, edits.ts, chips.ts, counts.ts, card.ts, draft.ts,
                                refusals.ts, repairs.ts, spans.ts, mark.ts
  src/lib/map/                  style.ts, fill.ts, project.ts, webgl.ts, library.ts
  src/lib/art/                  names.ts: every drawing by its name, with its size, its frames and where it
                                is cut. Generated by `gen:art`, committed, never edited
  src/lib/town/                 vibes.ts (the four vibes a town is drawn from, by id), plan.ts (the rule),
                                pieces.ts (which drawing lies where), bands.ts, said.ts, release.ts, town.ts,
                                and picture.ts, which puts a town together from the text, for tests alone
  src/lib/session/              session.ts: the search that is open, the areas to compare and the link
                                that was last made, in memory
  src/lib/compare/              list.ts: two to four areas, and the areas an address names
  src/lib/area/                 profile.ts (what goes under which heading), portrait.ts (where an area sits on
                                each vibe, read off the facts), describe.ts (title, description, canonical
                                address, structured data)
  src/lib/                      city.ts, paths.ts, headers.ts, format.ts, facts.ts, indexing.ts, vibes.ts,
                                holds.ts (what the release can answer), sight.ts (brings what a press opens
                                into sight)
  src/styles/                   tokens.css, base.css
  test/                         privacy/, access/, search/, area/, compare/, share/, census/, income/,
                                recorded.test.ts, tokens.test.ts, styles.test.ts, faces.test.ts, art.test.ts,
                                icon.test.ts, pictures.test.ts, pages.test.tsx, fault.test.tsx, indexing.test.tsx,
                                visit.test.tsx
  test/recorded/                answers captured from the API, one file a scenario, and index.json.
                                area/ holds every area's profile, visit/ one person's whole visit
  test/record.py                captures them
  test/support/                 the jsdom environment, the axe check, the contrast formula, the stand-ins
                                for the API and for the map library, the watch for leaks, the contract check,
                                visit.ts, which answers only what the recorded visit was sent, css.ts, which
                                reads a style sheet, and woff2.ts, which reads the file of a face
```

| Script | Does |
|---|---|
| `gen:api` | Writes `src/lib/api/schema.d.ts`, every API type, and `src/lib/api/required.ts`, what each answer must hold, from `contracts/openapi.json` |
| `check:api` | Generates both again and fails if a committed one differs |
| `gen:art` | Makes every picture of `public/art/` and the list `src/lib/art/names.ts` from the text of `art/`. The same text gives the same bytes on every machine. A drawing is made of the seventeen colours of the look, by their names, and of no other |
| `check:art` | Makes both again, in memory, and fails if a committed picture or the list differs, if a picture has no text, or if a text has no picture |
| `typecheck`, `lint`, `test` | `next typegen`, which writes the types Next generates, then `tsc --noEmit`. `eslint .`, with `no-console`, the accessibility rules and every kind of browser storage as errors, and no warning allowed. `jest` |
| `build`, `dev` | `next build`. `next dev`, which needs a port |
| `check:pages` | Reads every page in `.next/server/app` as it was built: its language and title, headings in order, landmarks, the link past them, a name for every link, field, button, drawing and table, ids, each banner on every page or on none, `noindex` while the data is made up or the release is a preview, an icon of the website's own, the face of names asked for ahead as a file that is there, no style sheet asked for ahead that no page lays, no filler and nothing left unfilled, and nothing from another origin. It counts what the search page holds before anything is opened (section 4.1), and what the page of an area draws at first (section 4.2): that it opens with the portrait and ends with where to go and look, that what is long is closed, and that no more is drawn than was measured. It holds every part that opens to be closed as built, and the page of vibes to have been built. It reads the robots file and the headers as they were built as well: that no crawler is kept out, and that every answer asks to be left out while the data is made up or the release is a preview. It leaves out `_global-error.html`, which is the framework's own (section 12) |
| `check` | `check:api`, `check:art`, `typecheck`, `lint`, `test`, `build`, `check:pages`, in that order, stopping at the first failure. It takes some four minutes: on 2026-09-26 it ran 5,063 tests in 169 suites, which took two and a half of them. On 2026-09-24 it took about a minute, with some 2,660 tests in 91 suites |

| Dependency | Why |
|---|---|
| `next` 16.3, `react` and `react-dom` 19.2 | The framework the plan names: static pages, server components, the build |
| `maplibre-gl` 5.24 | Draws the map. Version 6 has not been tried |
| `typescript` 5.9 | Type checking. Version 7 ships a native program, which the project does not depend on (ADR 0008). The generator needs 5, and the lint parser needs a version below 6.1 |
| `openapi-typescript` 7.6 | Generates the API types, so none is written by hand. `overrides` holds `@redocly/openapi-core`, which it depends on, at 1.30.0 |
| `eslint` 9, `eslint-config-next` | Lint, with the React, hooks and accessibility rules. Version 10 of `eslint` is not yet taken by the plugins that come with Next's rules |
| `jest`, `jest-environment-jsdom` | Tests, through `next/jest`. `check:pages` reads built pages with the parser that comes with it, and finds it through it |
| `@testing-library/react`, `/dom`, `/user-event`, `/jest-dom` | Tests that use a component as a person does |
| `axe-core` | Automated accessibility checks. `jest-axe` is not used: the tests call `axe-core` themselves |
| `ajv` | Checks each recorded answer against the contract, and each edit a control builds |
| `@maplibre/maplibre-gl-style-spec` 24.8 | Checks the map's style against MapLibre's own specification in tests. It is the package `maplibre-gl` already depends on, named so that a test may import it |
| `@types/react`, `@types/react-dom`, `@types/node`, `@types/jest` | Types for the above |

**The look added no dependency.** `package.json` gained two scripts, and `check` one step. What makes a picture writes the file of one itself. It packs the bytes in a way that is written down in it, since how Node's own `zlib` packs is left to the library a build of Node was made with, and a picture is checked on other machines than the one that made it. `zlib` reads every picture back, there and in `test/art.test.ts`. What reads the file of a face in the tests unpacks it with `node:zlib` too, and reads its tables itself. The two faces are files, and no package.

The first install, on 2026-09-23, confirmed the versions above. `package.json` holds each to its minor version, because no lockfile is committed (ADR 0008). `jest.config.ts` switches Jest's use of a file-watching service off, because a run hung where one was installed and ended at once with it off. It gives a test two minutes and not Jest's 5 seconds, because a test that draws the whole search page takes seconds, the slowest took 21 to 32 with three running at once, and a hosted runner is slower. It puts `test/support/maplibre.ts` in place of `maplibre-gl`, because jsdom has no WebGL. `jest.setup.ts` puts a plain link in place of Next's, which fetches pages ahead of time, makes jsdom's canvas answer as a browser with no WebGL does, keeps a failing test from printing the page, and gives a wait for the page five seconds and not the library's one, of which the field for a place spends a quarter before it asks.

**Recorded answers.** Each file in `test/recorded/` holds one request and its answer: `scenario`, `shows` (what it is there to show, in a line), `captured`, `request` (`operation_id`, `method`, `route`, `path` and, for a `POST`, `body`), `status`, `headers`, `body`. They are captured from the real app through its test client by `test/record.py`, run as `make web-record` from the repository root. The clock is fixed and the ids are seeded, so a second run writes the same bytes. The files are committed and never edited. Each scenario is there to show one state, and the script holds what that is as a test of the answer (`PROVES`): the reader of sentences changes, and a sentence that was a question one day was read in full the next, twice while the website was put together. When a scenario no longer shows its state the script writes nothing and names it, and its sentence is reworded until it does. `recorded.test` checks every `body` against the contract, by its operation and its status, so a change to the contract fails the test until they are captured again. Every file has been captured, and a test fails if one says `"captured": false`. `index.json` lists every scenario. `area/` holds the profile of every area by slug, so that every area page can be built with no API. Scenarios needed, of which all are recorded, with others beside them: `meta`, `areas`, `geometry`, `area`, `area-not-found`, `interpret-first`, `interpret-clarify`, `interpret-notice`, `interpret-nothing-read`, `interpret-degraded`, `interpret-rejected`, `interpret-unmet`, `rank-first`, `rank-refined`, `rank-nothing-matches`, `rank-stale-spec`, `explanations-first`, `compare-three`, `places-search`, `share-made`, `share-opened`, `share-not-found`, `share-gone`, `error-internal`. There are 160 in all. Added for slice 1: a vibe added from the shelf, a scale and a scale turned, a suggestion and one chosen, a suggestion beside a notice and of a place, more suggestions than are shown at first, a journey over its limit beside one with no time, a journey by bike over its limit, the reasons of a share, and, under `variant-a/`, the release where gritty is built as one part of a place. Added when the website was put together, each for a state that had been tested with an answer changed by hand: `interpret-by-model` and `interpret-off-topic` (the service with a stand-in for the model behind it), `rank-second-sentence` and `rank-switched-off` (a usual setting taken off, which leaves an entry of 0), `rank-on-foot` and `explanations-on-foot` (a journey beyond the longest time the release holds), `compare-crime`, and `share-made-no-place`.

**The visit.** `test/recorded/visit/` holds one person's whole visit, 29 steps, recorded by `record_the_visit`. The last seven are a search begun at the shelf: a word added, its scale turned from the chip, a sentence that is not plain, and what the page takes of the things that were noticed in it. Each request is made of the answer before it, and is the request the website sends at that step. `test/visit.test.tsx` walks the same visit through the pages and answers a request only if it is one of those to the letter, so it fails when the website sends what the service was not sent, and when a step was recorded that the website does not take. Every other test answers from a recording whatever was sent. This one is the nearest any test comes to running the website against the service. When the website changes what it sends, change `record_the_visit` to match and record again. **The visit was recorded again on the evening of 2026-09-26, as the website makes it since it asks nothing.** It had been recorded while a person chose, the second of the places that bear a name among what they chose, and the website takes the first. The whole walk is held again, and beside it a plain test holds its first steps. When what the website takes of a sentence changes, the visit is recorded again in the same change.

| Part | Owns | Hands to the others | Takes from the others |
|---|---|---|---|
| A. Data and shell | `src/lib/api`, `test/recorded/`, `test/support/`, `src/styles`, `src/content`, `city.ts`, `paths.ts`, the layout, the area, methods and sources pages, and the A components | The generated types. `client.ts`: one function a route, named by its operation id in camel case (`explainTop` for `explain_top`), that takes a body and an abort signal, never throws, and answers with `meta`, `data` and `synthetic`, or a failure. `recorded.ts`: `recordedAnswer`, `recordedError` and `responseFrom`, for tests. `faultsIn` in `test/support/axe.ts`. The tokens | Nothing |
| B. Search | `src/lib/search`, `src/lib/session`, `src/lib/compare`, `src/lib/vibes.ts`, the search, compare and share pages, the B components | The store of section 5.3 and its actions: `submitText`, `applyEdits`, `boxChanged`, `addPlace`, `setTenure`, `rankNow`, `retry`, `stop`, `startAgain`, `openShare`, `createShare`, `select`, `hover`, `openSettings`, `loadGeometry`. The session: `useCompare` and `useOpenSearch` | A's client, types, tokens and `FactRow`. C's `MapView`, `AreaTable` and primitives |
| C. Map and access | `src/lib/map`, the C components, `test/privacy`, `test/access` | `MapView` and `AreaTable` with the props of section 7. The test helpers: the canary run and the axe check | A's types and tokens. B's store, through props only |

## 12. What is left out, and how it attaches later

| Left out | How it attaches |
|---|---|
| "Go and look" beyond the stations: the town centre and the nearest large park by name, and one walk between them (vibes.md, section 5.3) | The API serves no fact for any of them (section 13, gap 27). Each is a new row of "Go and look" on an area's page, laid out from its fact as a station is |
| A search that starts from an area and keeps it as an anchor, so that "more like this" ranks by likeness | "Search for these vibes" adds the vibes the area has more of than most, and no more. An anchor is a new part of the spec (vibes.md, step 8), and is the engine's to add first |
| The portrait in a result, and the vibes of the areas most like an area set side by side on its page | A result has the strip. The areas most like an area each lead to the comparison of the two, where their vibes are side by side |
| A first result that is whole on the first screen after every search, and a page within the limits of section 4.1 | The strip of the map went under the first result, and on a phone the first result is whole after a plain search. It is not after every search, and in the look a card is more than twice as high as it was. What gives way is a decision of the design, and is not made here: the question of which place was meant, the chips, or what a card says before it is opened. [look.md](look.md) lists each under what is still open |
| Accounts and shortlists | **Accounts are built, and off until they are turned on** (section 2.3). A person who has signed in keeps a search, and no shortlist of areas is built. A "Save" slot is kept in the card's actions. A shortlist is a new slice of the store behind an interface, filled from new routes |
| Streaming explanations | `explanations` in the store is filled sentence by sentence through the same field. The card already holds a place for each sentence, so text that arrives late moves nothing |
| Sentences written by a model | The label for `origin: model` is built. Nothing else changes |
| A basemap | `buildStyle` takes a basemap: sources and layers go under the fill, the policy of section 10 gains the tile host, and the credit goes in the map's corner. No other file changes |
| Analytics | None in the browser, now or later. What is worth counting is counted by the API from its own call records |
| Remembering anything between visits | A theme, an offline copy: each needs storage, so each waits for a decision that changes section 10. A last search is kept for a person who has signed in and lets Burro keep it, at the service and never in the browser (section 2.3) |
| The look, on the iPhone app | The app is built, in `apps/ios`, to sections 3, 4 and 5 as they stood before 2026-09-26, which describe behaviour and not React. The same `contracts/openapi.json` generates its models. It has no tab to begin a search at, and its words are the website's as they were before they were written again. It has none of the look: its colours and its words are held to a copy of the website's files as they stood on 2026-09-26, until it is given the look ([ADR 0037](../adr/0037-the-iphone-app-is-held-to-a-copy-of-the-websites-colours-and-words.md)) |
| Dark colours | There is one look ([ADR 0033](../adr/0033-the-website-looks-like-the-map-of-a-gentle-game.md)). A dark look is a second value for each of the twenty colours, every pair of them checked again, and every drawing made in them |
| The city drawn as little towns on a grid, and anything on the map that the data does not hold | Not taken. The map is the real border of every area. A town is drawn of one area, beside its name, and never on the map |
| The town of an area on a result of a phone | A result under 36rem wide has no town: on a phone the first result has 16 px to spare, and a town is 64 px high. The other way is built, and one line chooses it ([ADR 0034](../adr/0034-the-town-of-an-area-is-drawn-by-rule-and-is-no-picture-of-the-place.md)) |
| Route detail, photos, the attributed excerpt | Fields on route 6 when the API has them, and new rows of `AreaProfile` |
| The page a fault in the server shows before anything is drawn | The framework builds it itself, as `_global-error.html`, whatever the website holds: it sets no language, carries no banner and is not in the website's words. It shows no figure and names no place. `global-error.tsx` is the website's own and is shown where the layout fails once the page is running. To replace the framework's page, add `src/pages/500.tsx` with a `_document` that sets the language, which brings the older router in for that one page |
| The website's check in `make ci` | `make web-check` runs it, and hosted CI runs it as a job of its own, beside `make ci`. It is not part of `make ci`, which needs no Node |

## 13. What the contract does not yet give the website

Each is a request to the backend. The website does what the last column says until it is met, and nothing here is worked round by writing a fact. The numbers are kept as they were, because tests and other documents name a gap by its number.

| # | Gap | Asked for | Until then |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | Route 3 serves only the facts a sentence cites, so a journey that is not a reason has no fact, and no source or date | Every `travel` and `budget_fit` fact of each explained area | The source and date of any `travel` fact in hand, because a release states them once for all journeys (contract section 2). With none in hand, "Source" links to `/sources` |
| 3 | Route 3 explains the first five areas and no others | `area_ids` on route 3 | Rows for results 6 to 20 (section 4), of which 6 to 10 are shown at first |
| 4 | A fact from route 6 has no sentence, and the crime caveat lives only in the contract | `text` on a `Fact` | `FactRow`, and the caveat copied into `src/content` with a test that holds it to the contract's words |
| 5 | No water and no destination points are served. No answer says where a place is: `places` holds its id, its name and its kind | Optional layers on route 5, and where each place of a search is, in `places` of routes 1, 2, 9 and 10 | The background stands for water. The places a person named are not marked on the map, because the website is not told where they are. The areas are named on it, from the release |
| 6 | Whether a model is configured is not known until route 1 answers | A flag on route 11 | The page of methods says which is so, from `reader` of route 11, and the search page asks the service as it opens and sends no sentence until it has said. Until the evening of 2026-09-26 the line under the box said it before anything was typed. Until that afternoon a line beside the chips said who had read the words, once they were read. Both are gone |
| 7 | The website's address must be in `BURRO_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` where the API runs | A deployment setting | `http://localhost:3000` only |
| 8 | Route 11 names the kinds of home and does not say which go with renting and which with buying. The reducer refuses one that does not suit the tenure | The kinds for each tenure, on route 11 | `SEGMENTS` in `src/content/settings.ts`, typed by the generated enum. A test holds it to the kinds the API serves a cost for |
| 9 | Route 6 serves what no fact holds: whether a station is step-free, an area's other names, its coordinates. None has a source or a date | A fact for each, or the field taken out | None is shown, on the page or in its structured data |
| 10 | Route 7 gives each area a status and no score, so a comparison cannot say how well each area fits. It now says how much of what counts each area has a figure for (`counted`, `present`), and the page shows it | `score` and `rank` on `ComparedArea` | The rank and the fit from the ranking of the search that is open, when it is of the same spec. With no search open, neither. Beside either, "It has a figure for 4 of the 10 things that count", from the API's counts |
| 12 | The website's own address is not known to the API or to the build | A deployment setting | `BURRO_SITE_URL`. With none set nothing may be indexed, whatever the release |
| 38 | A release says whether it is a preview (contract 2.1 and 9.1). The website shows a banner for one, and hears it from each answer as it hears `synthetic`. Route 11 serves every vibe the release carries, and the cutoff of every kind of journey, whatever the release holds. A preview holds no journey and no cost, and places an area on a vibe only where 60 in 100 of its recipe is measured: a first build does so for one vibe. So the shelf offers a vibe that no area has a band for, and such a vibe, once chosen, leaves every area unranked. The methods page prints a longest journey where none was routed. It was gap 23 until the vibes were joined, where that number was already taken | Met by engine 1.9.0. Route 11 says what the release holds (`holds`, `recipes`), the reducer refuses as `not_in_release` a vibe no area has a band for, a budget where no home of that kind has a cost and a journey where no place is named, and route 1 names what was asked for and is not there (`not_in_release`) | Section 3.1 |

**Met by contract version 2 and engine 1.4.0.** Each was a gap of this section. What the website did in the meantime is deleted.

| # | The gap was | Met by | What the website does now |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A spec held a `place_id` and no route gave its name | `places` in the answers of routes 1, 2, 9 and 10: the name of every place of the spec returned | Every place is shown by its name, from `places`. `PLACE.unnamed`, its hint, the `place_named` event and what `SharePanel` said of a place that stood in are deleted. The test that waited, `test_a_place_is_shown_by_its_name_and_never_by_a_number`, is now `test_every_place_the_person_named_is_shown_by_its_name_wherever_it_is_shown`, with no mark |
| 11 | Route 9 stored coarse places and did not name them | `places` in the answers of routes 9 and 10 | What a link holds is drawn from the names the answer gives |
| 14 | Route 3 returned no `spec_hash` | `spec_hash` in the `data` of route 3 | Reasons are held to the ranking by the hash their own answer names. The website keeps no hash of what it sent |
| 15 | Routes 4, 5, 6 and 11 could be answered from a browser's cache for an hour after a release changed | `Cache-Control: no-cache` with the `ETag` they carry | Nothing more. A browser asks each time. A built page is built again each hour (section 2) |
| 16 | A feature's `label` named a measure in the words of its publisher | `short_label` on each `Metric` and `Tag` | A switch and a chip are named by `short_label`. Methods and the working of a result name a feature by its `label` |
| 17 | A reason could be said from its bad side, a journey given as the trade-off did not say the limit it was over, and a thing worth a hair under a half could be the trade-off | Both sides of a standing in a fact, `limit` and `margin` in a `travel` fact, and `TRADE_OFF_MAX_UTILITY` | Every sentence is shown word for word, as before. The website's check of a trade-off reads `limits.trade_off_max_utility` from route 11. `test_no_reason_is_said_in_the_word_a_trade_off_is_said_in` passes, and its mark is off |
